<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49303202' data-tags='Web,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49303202' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcworld.com/article/3212428/firefox-is-now-the-last-major-browser-that-still-supports-ublock-origin.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Firefox is now the last major browser that still supports uBlock Origin</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Firefox 成为仍支持 uBlock Origin 的最后一款主流浏览器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 12:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49303202' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1694</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49303202' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 672</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49303202'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox remains the last major browser to support uBlock Origin, as others shift away from traditional ad-block extensions due to evolving extension policies. This preserves a familiar privacy tool for Firefox users but underscores growing fragmentation in how browsers handle ad blocking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 仍然是主流浏览器中最后一个支持 uBlock Origin 的选择。随着其他浏览器因扩展策略和 MV3 变更而削弱对这类扩展的支持，Firefox 的隐私保护能力仍具吸引力，但也暴露出浏览器扩展生态的分化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.pcworld.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/3212428-0-62401400-1786636364-Firefox-foto-mozilla.png?w=1024' data-full='https://www.pcworld.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/3212428-0-62401400-1786636364-Firefox-foto-mozilla.png?w=1024' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen 3.8-27B FP8 提供更高效的推理，27亿参数在普通显卡上也能更快地跑，显存需求更低，便于个人和小团队尝试大模型。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49272832' data-tags='Programming,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49272832' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tailscale.com/blog/sqlite-wal-reset-bug' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tracking down the 16-year-old WAL-reset SQLite bug</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>追踪这起持续16年的 SQLite WAL 重置漏洞</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 07:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49272832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1188</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49272832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 229</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49272832'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers zero in on a long-standing SQLite WAL reset bug that has persisted for 16 years. The issue can cause data integrity problems when the write-ahead logging file resets under certain conditions, impacting apps using WAL mode. The takeaway is to test WAL edge cases and ensure robust backup/restore paths, highlighting SQLite quirks in real-world apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员锁定一宗持续了16年的 SQLite WAL 重置漏洞。该问题在特定条件下会导致写入前日志文件重置时的数据完整性问题，影响使用 WAL 模式的应用程序。建议开发者测试 WAL 的边界情况，并确保备份/恢复路径健全，凸显实际应用中 SQLite 的潜在坑点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tailscale.com/api/og-image?id=45389fc1-da6e-4e0d-82f6-140651c6ba57' data-full='https://tailscale.com/api/og-image?id=45389fc1-da6e-4e0d-82f6-140651c6ba57' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Muse Glimmer：面向持续本地代理工作流优化的30B参数模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 03:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49241679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1172</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49241679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 634</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49241679'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Muse Glimmer is a 30B-parameter model optimized for always-on local agent workflows, enabling persistent autonomous agents with privacy-friendly, low-latency inference. It embodies a shift toward on-device or edge AI that reduces reliance on cloud services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Muse Glimmer 是一个为持续本地代理工作流优化的 30B 参数模型，支持持续自我驱动的代理并具备隐私友好、低延迟的推理能力。这反映了在本地或边缘部署 AI、减少对云端依赖的趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国将全面禁止未经同意的电话推销</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 01:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49254880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1035</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49254880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 487</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49254880'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>France plans to ban unsolicited telemarketing calls starting August 11, tightening consumer protection. The policy could reshape how marketers reach customers, boosting opt-in channels and call-blocking solutions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国将于11日起全面禁止未经请求的电话推销，强化对消费者隐私的保护。该举措可能促使企业转向获同意的联系渠道，并影响营销行业的电话营销策略。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.florianherrengt.com/ai-removing-middle-class-software-engineering.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is removing the middle class of software engineering?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 正在吞并软件工程的中产阶层</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 06:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49271994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 976</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49271994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 904</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49271994'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A viewpoint argues that AI is eroding the middle tier of software engineering by automating routine tasks and raising productivity. It questions career ladders and suggests that engineers must adapt by focusing on higher-level design, system architecture, and AI-augmented workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观点认为 AI 正通过自动化日常任务和提高生产率，削弱软件工程的中层岗位。文章讨论职业晋升路径的变化，建议工程师在高层设计、系统架构以及与 AI 协作的工作流中寻找新机会。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49289112'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemini 3.7 Flash extends the Gemini line with a lighter, faster variant optimized for low latency and smaller memory usage. It underscores Google’s ongoing push to edge-ready AI and developer-friendly tooling for large models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemini 3.7 快闪版在保持核心能力的同时，提供更轻量、低延迟的推理性能，降低内存占用，便于边缘部署。此举显示谷歌在大模型的边缘化与开发者工具方面的持续推进。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49250836' data-tags='Data,Privacy,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49250836' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thewalrus.ca/google-search-is-dying/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>As AI eats the web, the internet’s collective memory is disappearing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>随着 AI 吃掉网络，互联网的集体记忆正在消失</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 15:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49250836' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 910</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49250836' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 937</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49250836'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As AI consumes more of the web, the internet’s collective memory is thinning. The piece argues that pervasive AI crawling and caching threaten provenance and long-term memory, complicating future search and verification. It calls for durable archiving and mindful data sourcing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着 AI 越学越多地抓取网络，互联网的集体记忆正在变薄。文章指出 AI 抓取与缓存的长期可追溯性下降，影响可验证的知识来源。呼吁加强长期存档和负责任的数据获取。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49296740' data-tags='Programming,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49296740' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mun-logadan.github.io/why-does-opus-5-feel-worse/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why does Opus 5 feel worse to work with?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么 Opus 5 的使用感更差？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 03:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49296740' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 902</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49296740' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 808</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49296740'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis argues that Opus 5 feels worse to work with due to UX and workflow frictions common in enterprise software. It points to inconsistent interfaces, onboarding friction, and brittle customization that hamper daily productivity, despite feature-rich capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Opus 5 的工作体验为何较差进行了分析，指向用户体验不一致、工作流阻塞和隐藏的复杂性等问题，影响日常生产力。该案例也折射出企业级软件在可用性方面的不足，即便功能强大也难以落地。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Every Fucking Website is a 2020 project that curates a vast directory of sites, reflecting the sheer scale of the web. It serves as a provocative reference for web exploration and raises questions about discoverability in a trillion-page ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Every Fucking Website》是 2020 年的一个汇集大量网站的项目，体现了网络的庞大规模。它为网络探索提供一种挑衅性的参照，也引发了在海量站点中如何发现内容的思考。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49234675' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49234675' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://laurentiugabriel.github.io/blog/articles/how-i-use-llms-to-learn/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How I use LLMs to learn complex topics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我如何用大语言模型学习复杂主题</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 12:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49234675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 804</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49234675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 531</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49234675'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>I use a staged approach to learning with LLMs: map an outline with the model, generate concise explanations, then test understanding with targeted prompts. This turns the model into a structured tutor that surfaces gaps and helps build solid mental models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将学习分成阶段使用模型：用它勾勒大纲、生成简明解释、再通过有针对性的提问检验理解。把模型当作高效的学习辅导工具，帮助建立稳固的认知模型并暴露薄弱点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://laurentiugabriel.github.io/blog/images/ChipTycoon.png' data-full='https://laurentiugabriel.github.io/blog/images/ChipTycoon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek Harness appears to be a framework around DeepSeek AI to run experiments and benchmark performance. It could help teams standardize evaluation, compare model behavior, and improve reproducibility in AI workflows. This development comes at a time when measurable benchmarks are increasingly important for agent-powered systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek Harness 似乎是围绕 DeepSeek AI 的一个用于实验运行与性能基准的框架。它有助于团队标准化评估、对比模型行为，并提升 AI 工作流的可重复性。随着对代理系统的基准衡量需求提升，这类工具的重要性也在上升。</p>

The result underscores how hardware-software co-design can dramatically shrink response times for cutting-edge LLMs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cerebras 在 Sol Ultrafast 平台上为 GPT‑5.6 提供显著加速，结合定制硬件与软件优化以缩短推理时间。硬件与软件的协同设计再次推动下一代大模型的实用性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/xoreaxeaxeax/skitter-creek-bath-salts' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spaghettifying DRAM</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 07:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49286341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 701</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49286341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49286341'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spaghettifying DRAM examines how unusual or chaotic memory layouts can affect latency, bandwidth, and reliability. The discussion highlights the ongoing tension between memory hardware complexity and software efficiency, suggesting approaches for hardware-aware optimization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Spaghettifying DRAM 探讨了非传统、混乱的内存布局如何影响延迟、带宽与可靠性。该讨论凸显了内存硬件复杂性与软件效率之间的矛盾，并提出面向硬件的优化思路。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49257876' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stolen-thoughts.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stealing Reasoning Traces from Proprietary LLM APIs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从专有大语言模型 API 窃取推理痕迹</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 06:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49257876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 686</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49257876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 301</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49257876'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate that proprietary LLM APIs can leak traces of reasoning, exposing intermediate prompts and steps. This raises privacy and security concerns about using hosted models and underscores the need for defenses against leakage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者展示了专有大语言模型 API 可能泄露推理痕迹，暴露中间提示和推理步骤。这对使用托管模型的隐私与安全构成挑战，凸显防止信息泄露的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stolen-thoughts.com/assets/fig1-extraction-fidelity.svg' data-full='https://stolen-thoughts.com/assets/fig1-extraction-fidelity.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49239751' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.docker.com/products/docker-sandboxes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Docker Sandboxes – Disposable, isolated sandboxes for AI agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Docker 沙盒：面向 AI 代理的一次性隔离沙箱</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 23:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49239751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 663</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49239751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 368</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49239751'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Docker Sandboxes provide disposable, isolated environments for running AI agents, enabling safer experimentation and reproducibility. They offer controlled dependencies and snapshot-like configurations to prevent cross-agent interference. This helps teams prototype AI tasks without risking the host system.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Docker Sandboxes 提供一次性、隔离的运行环境，专为 AI 代理设计，降低实验风险并提高可重复性。通过可控依赖和快照式配置，开发者可以在不影响宿主系统的情况下安全试验复杂的 AI 任务。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.docker.com/app/uploads/2024/02/docker-default-meta-image-1110x583.png' data-full='https://www.docker.com/app/uploads/2024/02/docker-default-meta-image-1110x583.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49232138' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49232138' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.notebookcheck.net/Windows-11-s-built-in-Weather-app-wastes-more-than-1-GB-of-RAM.1364205.0.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Windows 11&#x27;s built-in Weather app wastes more than 1 GB of RAM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows 11 内置天气应用耗费超过 1GB RAM</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 08:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 653</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 581</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49232138'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A review finds the built-in Weather app consumes over a gigabyte of RAM, highlighting how preinstalled apps can bloat systems and affect performance even on modern hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>评测发现 Windows 11 内置天气应用的内存占用超过 1GB，凸显系统自带应用的资源占用及对性能的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.notebookcheck.net/fileadmin/Notebooks/News/_nc5/Windows_11_01.jpg' data-full='https://www.notebookcheck.net/fileadmin/Notebooks/News/_nc5/Windows_11_01.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49251411' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49251411' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.effort.news/uk-lobby' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The UK&#x27;s war on anonymity has come to America</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国对匿名性的打压蔓延至美国</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 16:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49251411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 652</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49251411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 748</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49251411'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A UK-style push to curb online anonymity is gaining traction in the US, with lawmakers favoring identity verification and traceability. The move signals a broader shift toward user accountability that could reshape platform moderation, data sharing, and civil liberties. For technologists, the takeaway is that privacy-centered design may need stronger defaults and clearer opt-outs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国对匿名性的打压正在美国获得关注，立法者日益支持身份核验和可追溯性。此举预示着向用户问责的全球性潮流，可能影响平台治理、数据共享和公民自由。对技术人来说，隐私友好设计需要更强的默认保护与清晰的选择权。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.effort.news/images/article-graphics/uk-lobby/reset-bipartite-graph-cover.png' data-full='https://www.effort.news/images/article-graphics/uk-lobby/reset-bipartite-graph-cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49263497' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49263497' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ngrok.com/blog/compression-is-prediction' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Compression is prediction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>压缩即预测</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 12:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49263497' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 651</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49263497' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 283</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49263497'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Data compression is fundamentally about predicting the next symbol; improving a compressor equates to a better predictor.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数据压缩本质上是对下一个符号的预测；提升压缩效果等同于改进预测能力。这一观点对机器学习、数据存储与推理具有实际启示，适用于希望把压缩思路融入模型设计的开发者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ngrok.com/blog-assets/images/2026-08-11-compression-is-prediction/og.png' data-full='https://ngrok.com/blog-assets/images/2026-08-11-compression-is-prediction/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49243880' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49243880' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/4e3957f8-ea7c-4c46-a3de-cdce8e526878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mark Zuckerberg attacks &#x27;closed&#x27; AI rivals as Meta returns to open models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>扎克伯格抨击封闭 AI 对手，Meta 重回开放模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 07:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49243880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 636</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49243880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 598</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49243880'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mark Zuckerberg frames Meta’s pivot toward open-weight AI models as a stance against closed rivals, signaling a push for open standards and modular AI. The move underscores the industry tension between proprietary systems and open collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>马克·扎克伯格将 Meta 的策略定位为对抗封闭式 AI 竞争对手，推动采用开放权重模型。此举体现了对开放标准与模块化 AI 的重视，以及行业在开放与封闭之间的博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2F8b2e6973-50e7-406b-8076-5605a762cb0f.jpg?source=next-barrier-page&amp;fit=scale-down&amp;quality=highest&amp;width=700&amp;dpr=1' data-full='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2F8b2e6973-50e7-406b-8076-5605a762cb0f.jpg?source=next-barrier-page&amp;fit=scale-down&amp;quality=highest&amp;width=700&amp;dpr=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49274027' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49274027' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://x.ai/news/grok-4-6' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Grok 4.6</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Grok 4.6</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 08:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49274027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 627</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49274027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 606</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49274027'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Grok 4.6 updates the AI assistant with refinements to conversational ability, integration options, and developer-facing tooling. The release highlights ongoing efforts to evolve AI assistants into more capable, context-aware productivity partners.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Grok 4.6 对AI助手进行了若干改进，提升对话能力并扩展集成与开发工具。此次发布凸显了让AI助手成为更具情境感知的高效生产力伙伴的持续努力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://x.ai/images/news/grok-4-6-og.png' data-full='https://x.ai/images/news/grok-4-6-og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49273165' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49273165' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andrewpwheeler.com/2026/08/12/license-plate-reader-searches-should-require-a-warrant/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>License plate reader searches should require a warrant</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>车牌识别搜索应当获得授权才可进行</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 07:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49273165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 627</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49273165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 380</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49273165'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Policy arguments are made for requiring warrants to search license plate data collected by LPR systems. It argues targeted, lawfully justified access over generic bulk surveillance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出在使用车牌识别（LPR）系统检索数据时应当获得搜查令的政策主张。文章强调公民自由与滥用风险、数据保留与共享的隐私边界，以及应优先实现有针对性且合法授权的访问，而非大规模监控。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://andrewpwheeler.com/wp-content/uploads/2017/06/apwface.png?w=200' data-full='https://andrewpwheeler.com/wp-content/uploads/2017/06/apwface.png?w=200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49242739' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49242739' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bobdahacker.com/blog/tldv-hack' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tl;dv: Over 180k meetings left wide open</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tl;dv：超过18万次会议被暴露</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 05:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49242739' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 622</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49242739' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 206</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49242739'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tl;dv reveals that over 180,000 meetings were left exposed due to misconfigured access controls, raising concerns about the privacy of meeting transcripts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tl;dv 指出有超过 18 万场会议因访问控制配置不当而暴露，凸显会议记录隐私的潜在风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bobdahacker.com/static/images/blogs/tldv/blogHeader.png' data-full='https://bobdahacker.com/static/images/blogs/tldv/blogHeader.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49270726' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49270726' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://digitalescapetools.com/2026/08/ublock-origin-stops-chasing-facebook-ads.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>uBlock Origin Is Giving Up the Fight to Keep Ads Off Facebook</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>uBlock Origin 放弃对 Facebook 广告的拦截</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 04:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49270726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 611</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49270726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 726</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49270726'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>uBlock Origin reportedly stops fighting Facebook ads as Facebook arms itself with anti-adblock techniques. The move shifts the burden to site owners and raises questions about the future of comprehensive ad blocking on social networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>称为 Facebook 引入对抗广告拦截措施后，uBlock Origin 不再继续拦截 Facebook 广告。此举将拦截责任转向站点所有者，并引发对社交平台上全面广告屏蔽未来的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49231154' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49231154' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.terrygodier.com/2026/08/09/mea-culpa-dark-hours.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mea Culpa – Dark Hours</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>致歉信：黑暗时刻</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 06:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49231154' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 602</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49231154' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 279</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49231154'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An intimate, candid mea culpa written during a dark chapter. It acknowledges mistakes and their consequences, then outlines concrete changes the author intends to make. The post emphasizes accountability and learning from hardship rather than excuses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇在艰难时期写就的坦诚自我批评。文中承认了错误及其后果，并提出今后将采取的具体改进措施，强调对所发生事件的负责与从中学习的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.terrygodier.com/assets/logo.png' data-full='https://blog.terrygodier.com/assets/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49268580' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49268580' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.asus.com/accessories/bike-booster/asus-oxiis/oxiis-intelligent-bike-booster/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Asus Bike Booster</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>华硕自行车智能助力器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 23:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49268580' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 597</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49268580' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 419</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49268580'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Asus introduces Oxiis, an intelligent bike booster that attaches to a regular bicycle to provide adaptive pedal-assist and telemetry. Through smart power modulation and app integration, it aims to smooth both commuting and leisure rides.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>华硕推出 Oxiis 自行车智能助力器，安装在普通自行车上即可提供自适应踏板助力并带有数据遥测。通过智能调节动力并与应用联动，提升通勤和休闲骑行的平顺性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dlcdnwebimgs.asus.com/gain/36fca81d-66d4-4a3a-9fa3-05976be35494/' data-full='https://dlcdnwebimgs.asus.com/gain/36fca81d-66d4-4a3a-9fa3-05976be35494/' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49312845' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49312845' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://davidepiffer.com/p/ai-isnt-outthinking-mathematicians' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI isn’t outthinking mathematicians, it’s out-remembering them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 不是在超越数学家，而是在超越他们的记忆力</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 15, 2026 / 11:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49312845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 561</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49312845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 470</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49312845'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The argument is that AI&#x27;s edge lies in memorization and recall of mathematical facts, databases, and patterns, rather than deep inference. This reframes how we think about AI&#x27;s role in mathematical discovery and automated reasoning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观点认为 AI 在数学领域的优势不在于真正“超越”人类的思维，而在于记忆和检索海量的事实、模式与定理。此看法会影响我们对 AI 在数学发现与自动推理中角色的理解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!DIxw!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F2e2e14e7-c536-49c0-9f27-79b1073dd527_1279x759.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!DIxw!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F2e2e14e7-c536-49c0-9f27-79b1073dd527_1279x759.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49319556' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49319556' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/release-notes/system-prompts' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude: System Prompts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude：系统提示</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 05:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49319556' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 561</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49319556' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49319556'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude introduces new capabilities around system prompts, enabling more reliable persona control and safety constraints across conversations. The update emphasizes persistent or configurable prompts to steer model behavior without altering user prompts. This matters for developers building chatbots who require predictable, policy-compliant outputs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 引入了系统提示的新能力，使对话中的角色与行为更加可控和安全。更新强调可配置的系统提示，以在不改动用户提示的前提下引导模型行为。对于需要可预测、合规输出的聊天机器人开发者尤为重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.claude.com/docs/og?locale=en&amp;path=release-notes/system-prompts&amp;design-rev=1' data-full='https://platform.claude.com/docs/og?locale=en&amp;path=release-notes/system-prompts&amp;design-rev=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49257377' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49257377' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c75gk620r22o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>England set to be one of the first countries to eliminate hepatitis C</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英格兰有望成为首批根除丙型肝炎的国家之一</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 05:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49257377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 559</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49257377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 400</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49257377'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>England is poised to become one of the first countries to eliminate hepatitis C, a milestone driven by broad screening and highly effective antiviral treatments. If achieved, it would validate scalable public-health strategies and could influence targets for other nations facing HCV challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英格兰有望成为首批根除丙型肝炎的国家之一，这一成就依赖广泛筛查和高效抗病毒治疗的组合。若实现，将验证可复制的公共卫生策略，并可能激励其他国家设定相似目标。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/f1b4/live/9d52a710-94e4-11f1-a7ab-8b30adf0800a.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/f1b4/live/9d52a710-94e4-11f1-a7ab-8b30adf0800a.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49319633' data-tags='Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49319633' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://support.mozilla.org/en-US/kb/block-ads-firefox-ios' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Firefox for iOS now has a native adblocker</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>iOS 版 Firefox 现已内置原生广告拦截器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 05:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49319633' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 552</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49319633' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 229</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49319633'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox iOS now ships with a native ad-blocker, improving privacy and page speed by blocking trackers and ads at the browser level. This reduces data usage and helps users bypass some invasive tracking without relying on external extensions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>iOS 版 Firefox 增添原生广告拦截功能，通过浏览器层面屏蔽跟踪与广告来提升隐私和页面加载速度。减少数据使用，并在无需外部扩展的情况下改善用户体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://support.mozilla.org/_fs-ch-1T1wmsGaOgGaSxcX/assets/errorIcon.svg' data-full='https://support.mozilla.org/_fs-ch-1T1wmsGaOgGaSxcX/assets/errorIcon.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49246804' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Show HN'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49246804' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cactuscompute.com/needle' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Needle2: 14MB agentic LLM for phones, wearables, smart home and robots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Needle2：14MB 的设备端智能代理型大模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 10:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49246804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 522</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49246804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 176</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49246804'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Needle2 is a 14MB agentic LLM designed for phones, wearables, smart home devices, and robots, enabling on-device autonomous agents. It emphasizes privacy, low latency, and edge personalization by running entirely locally. Takeaway: ultra-compact LLMs are unlocking on-device AI for consumer devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Needle2 是一款面向手机、穿戴设备、智能家居和机器人等设备端运行的14MB代理型大模型，能够在本地实现自主代理。它强调隐私、低延迟和边缘个性化。要点在于超小模型正在推动边缘端 AI 的落地。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cactuscompute.com/opengraph-image?9df76a35fe8276e2' data-full='https://cactuscompute.com/opengraph-image?9df76a35fe8276e2' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49257160' data-tags='Business,Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49257160' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/e49dfb75-f841-4466-a577-f7aaff8779a0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI’s head of ethics leaves less than a year after joining</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 道德与伦理主管任职不足一年即离职</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 05:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49257160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 515</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49257160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 478</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49257160'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI&#x27;s chief ethics officer resigns after less than a year, signaling volatility in steering responsible AI at a leading lab. The move may recalibrate internal governance and external trust around AI safety, policy, and company commitments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 的伦理负责人在不到一年任期后离职，揭示这家研究机构在负责任 AI 的治理上面临的变动。此举可能影响内部治理与外部对 AI 安全、政策与承诺的信任度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2F9c0f12b6-f040-44b5-96a2-4c1e7ec6bd1d.jpg?source=next-barrier-page&amp;fit=scale-down&amp;quality=highest&amp;width=700&amp;dpr=1' data-full='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2F9c0f12b6-f040-44b5-96a2-4c1e7ec6bd1d.jpg?source=next-barrier-page&amp;fit=scale-down&amp;quality=highest&amp;width=700&amp;dpr=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49226636' data-tags='Hardware,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49226636' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://seg6.space/posts/phone-server/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My server is a phone now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的服务器现在是一部手机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 08, 2026 / 15:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49226636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 504</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49226636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 241</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49226636'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>People are running a server on a smartphone, using the device&#x27;s always-on nature and affordable hardware to host small services. This underlines edge computing feasibility with commodity devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将手机作为服务器的实践，展示用消费级设备承载小型服务的可行性。讨论的权衡包括电池、存储、操作系统支持和网络暴露时的安全性，并给出保持轻量、响应快速的实用建议。这也凸显了边缘计算在普通设备上的潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://seg6.space/img/phone-server/system-dashboard.png' data-full='https://seg6.space/img/phone-server/system-dashboard.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49270953' data-tags='Science,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49270953' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jonty.github.io/2026_eclipse_webcams/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>2026 Eclipse Webcams</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2026 年日食网络摄像头</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 04:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49270953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 500</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49270953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49270953'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Coincident with the 2026 eclipse, a collection of live webcams aggregates feeds from observatories and public viewing sites. The roundup helps observers plan timings, compare streams, and prepare for potential bandwidth spikes during peak moments. It’s a handy resource for remote viewers who want real-time visuals without leaving home.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>与2026年日食同时上线的多路直播摄像头汇集了来自观测站与公共观测点的画面。该清单帮助观众规划观看时间、比较画面质量，并预测日食高光时段的带宽压力。对于希望在家远程观看的用户，这是一个实用的实时画面资源。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49300314' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49300314' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/security/how-google-is-making-private-ai-practical-with-homomorphic-encryption/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google is making private AI practical with homomorphic encryption</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌通过同态加密让私有 AI 变得可实用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 08:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49300314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 488</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49300314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 282</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49300314'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google is pursuing practical private AI by using homomorphic encryption to run computations on encrypted data. This approach aims to unlock privacy-preserving AI in cloud and on-device settings, though it remains challenging in terms of performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌正通过同态加密让隐私保护的 AI 更具可用性，能够在加密数据上执行推理。该思路有望在云端和本地场景中保护用户数据隐私，但在性能方面仍存在挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Untitled_design_10_p3wN1dg.width-1300.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Untitled_design_10_p3wN1dg.width-1300.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49311651' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49311651' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alz-journals.onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/10.1002/dad2.70432' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Semaglutide linked to lower predicted dementia risk</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西马鲁肽与较低的预测性痴呆风险相关</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 15, 2026 / 08:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49311651' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 475</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49311651' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 377</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49311651'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New study links semaglutide use to a lower predicted dementia risk, according to risk-model analyses. The finding adds to growing interest in GLP-1 therapies as potential neuroprotectants, but it does not prove causation and leaves room for confounding factors. More research, including clinical dementia outcomes, is needed to confirm any causal effect.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示使用西马鲁肽与预测的痴呆风险下降相关，基于风险评分/生物标志物的分析。该结果提高了对GLP-1治疗在神经保护方面潜力的兴趣，但并不能证明因果关系，需进一步临床结果与机制研究来证实。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49269818' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49269818' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.abc.net.au/news/2026-08-06/ragebait-how-facebook-is-paying-controversial-creators/106940696' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Controversial creators are benefiting from monetization programs run by Meta</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>争议性创作者从 Meta 的变现计划获利</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 02:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49269818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 474</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49269818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 331</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49269818'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta’s monetization programs reportedly pay controversial creators, illustrating the tension between engagement-driven revenue and platform moderation. The arrangement raises questions about incentives, content policy, and where to draw the line between free expression and harmful rhetoric. For builders and platform operators, it underscores the need for transparent rules and auditing of funded content.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称 Meta 的变现计划向争议性创作者支付报酬，凸显以参与度驱动的创收与平台治理之间的张力。这一安排引发关于激励机制、内容政策以及言论自由与有害言论界限的讨论。对于开发者和平台运营者而言，强调了规则透明和对受资助内容进行审计的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://live-production.wcms.abc-cdn.net.au/181e08c853385e4663d404d34dbfb1df?impolicy=wcms_watermark_news&amp;cropH=766&amp;cropW=1362&amp;xPos=379&amp;yPos=202&amp;width=862&amp;height=485&amp;imformat=generic' data-full='https://live-production.wcms.abc-cdn.net.au/181e08c853385e4663d404d34dbfb1df?impolicy=wcms_watermark_news&amp;cropH=766&amp;cropW=1362&amp;xPos=379&amp;yPos=202&amp;width=862&amp;height=485&amp;imformat=generic' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49268564' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49268564' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cringebot3000.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LinkedIn CringeBot 3000</a></p>

<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/ce97mm3vvemo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Count Binface receives over a quarter of votes in Clacton by-election</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Count Binface 在 Clacton 补选中获逾四分之一票</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 09:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49301260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 465</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49301260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 382</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49301260'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Satirical candidate Count Binface captured more than a quarter of the vote in the Clacton by-election, signaling appetite for anti-establishment voices. The result underscores how protest candidacies can influence local race dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讽刺性候选人 Count Binface 在 Clacton 补选中获得超过四分之一的选票，显示选民对反建制声音的需求上升。此结果也凸显了抗议候选人如何影响地方选举格局。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://community.openai.com/t/codex-in-chatgpt-desktop-app-for-linux-is-now-in-preview/1390027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codex in ChatGPT desktop app for Linux is now in preview</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ChatGPT 桌面应用在 Linux 上的 Codex 功能现已进入预览</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 21:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49281916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 458</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49281916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 311</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49281916'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Codex is now available in preview inside the Linux version of the ChatGPT desktop app, enabling code-focused assistance in the local environment. This marks a step toward broader Codex integration across desktop platforms and could speed up coding tasks for Linux users. Expect refinements as feedback rolls in during the preview.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 将 Codex 能力带入 Linux 版 ChatGPT 桌面应用，现已进入预览阶段。这让 Linux 用户在桌面环境中体验代码生成功能，标志着桌面端 AI 工具生态的扩展。随着反馈迭代，后续版本将进一步完善。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49304447' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49304447' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cryptographyengineering.com/2026/08/14/everything-is-about-to-go-dark/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Going Dark, and the era of law enforcement hacking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑暗来临：执法黑客时代</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 13:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49304447' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 449</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49304447' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 241</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49304447'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As encryption deepens, law enforcement increasingly relies on hacking techniques and sanctioned exploits to access data. The piece argues that the tension between security, privacy, and public safety is intensifying, shaping policy and tool development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着端到端加密的普及，执法机构越来越多地转向入侵与滥用漏洞来获取数据。这一趋势将隐私保护、公共安全与政策制定之间的矛盾推向前台，影响工具与法规的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/blog.cryptographyengineering.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/thewire.jpg?fit=1200%2C695&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/blog.cryptographyengineering.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/thewire.jpg?fit=1200%2C695&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49250109' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49250109' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://support.claude.com/en/articles/16266773-how-claude-marks-ai-generated-content' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Claude marks AI-generated content</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 如何标注 AI 生成的内容</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 14:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49250109' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 438</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49250109' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 399</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49250109'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude marks AI-generated content with explicit labeling to help users distinguish AI from human authors. This transparency can improve trust and guide moderation in mixed workflows, from content creation to Q&amp;A. The takeaway: look for clear AI-origin cues in Claude outputs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 将 AI 生成的内容以明确标签标注，帮助用户区分人类与机器作者。这种透明度有助于提高信任度，并在混合工作流中为内容审核提供指引。要点是：关注 Claude 输出中的 AI 来源标记。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://downloads.intercomcdn.com/i/o/lupk8zyo/792494/717b10d3ebd874823db64841659b/76f3062d78ebbb04863fb1de3ef9cca0.png' data-full='https://downloads.intercomcdn.com/i/o/lupk8zyo/792494/717b10d3ebd874823db64841659b/76f3062d78ebbb04863fb1de3ef9cca0.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49261133' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developers.googleblog.com/why-go-is-an-ideal-language-for-ai-assisted-software-engineering/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Go is an ideal language for AI-assisted software engineering</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何 Go 是 AI 辅助软件工程的理想语言</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 09:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49261133' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 431</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49261133' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 520</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49261133'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Go&#x27;s simplicity, strong concurrency, fast compilation, and reliability make it well-suited for building AI-assisted software tooling, such as code analysis, automations, and integration pipelines. The post argues Go&#x27;s ecosystem and predictable performance help teams ship AI-powered developer tools with fewer surprises.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Go 的简洁性、强大的并发模型、快速编译和稳定性使其非常适合构建 AI 辅助的开发工具，如代码分析、自动化和集成管道。文章认为 Go 的生态和可预测性能有助于团队更稳妥地发布 AI 驱动的开发者工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-developer-goog-blog-assets/images/Go_Logo_-_Blue.2e16d0ba.fill-1200x600.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-developer-goog-blog-assets/images/Go_Logo_-_Blue.2e16d0ba.fill-1200x600.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49252179' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49252179' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/antirez/h3.c' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>H3-metal – Native MiniMax-H3 inference for Apple Silicon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>H3-metal — 面向 Apple Silicon 的本地 MiniMax-H3 推理</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 18:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49252179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 431</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49252179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49252179'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Native MiniMax-H3 inference runs directly on Apple Silicon via H3-metal. It targets efficient, GPU-accelerated performance for H3 workloads on macOS, reducing cross-OS friction and CPU load. This is relevant for developers who want local, fast inference on Macs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>H3-metal 将 MiniMax-H3 的推理本地化到 Apple Silicon，借助 Metal 加速实现更高效的推理。目标是在 macOS 上提供更高效的推理性能，减少跨平台移植的负担和 CPU 开销。对于需要在 Mac 上本地快速处理 H3 相关工作流的开发者尤为有用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/82307ef7c3586906c8134620f66638f2035dae0e3bb5540abe159774ad0fe46e/antirez/h3.c' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/82307ef7c3586906c8134620f66638f2035dae0e3bb5540abe159774ad0fe46e/antirez/h3.c' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49309549' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49309549' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sankalp.bearblog.dev/autoresearch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Auto-research with codex: How I achieved a 232x Faster Kernel</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Codex 自动化研究：我如何让内核速度提升 232 倍</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 15, 2026 / 04:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49309549' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 430</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49309549' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49309549'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>I built an automated research workflow using Codex that accelerates kernel development by 232x. By combining AI-assisted exploration, targeted prompts, and automated code refinement, iteration cycles become dramatically faster for low-level systems work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>我用 Codex 构建了一个自动化研究工作流，将内核开发的迭代速度提升了 232 倍。通过结合 AI 辅助的探索、定向提示和自动代码改进，低级系统开发的迭代周期变得极大地提速。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sankalp.bearblog.dev/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://sankalp.bearblog.dev/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49290299' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49290299' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.geoffreylitt.com/2026/07/02/understanding-is-the-new-bottleneck' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Understanding is the new bottleneck</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>理解力成为新的瓶颈</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 11:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49290299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 429</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49290299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 237</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49290299'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As systems scale, human understanding of data, models, and outcomes becomes the limiting factor. The post argues that tooling for interpretability and governance will determine how fast teams can move from insight to action. Without better explainability, even powerful models can&#x27;t reliably translate gains into business value.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着系统规模扩大，对数据、模型和结果的理解成为制约因素。文章认为，提升可解释性和治理能力的工具将决定团队如何快速把洞察转化为行动。若缺乏更好的可解释性，强大的模型也难以可靠地把收益转化为商业价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://geoffreylitt.com/images/talks/understanding-bottleneck/card.jpg' data-full='https://geoffreylitt.com/images/talks/understanding-bottleneck/card.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49261128' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49261128' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.modular.com/blog/modular-26-5-mojo-1-0-is-here' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mojo 1.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mojo 1.0 发布</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 09:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49261128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 426</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49261128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 236</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49261128'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Modular announces Mojo 1.0, marking the language&#x27;s transition from preview to a stable release. Mojo combines Python-like ergonomics with a systems-level performance model, targeting AI/ML workloads and high-throughput apps, with a growing toolchain and libraries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Modular 宣布 Mojo 1.0 正式发布，标志着这门面向 AI/ML 及高吞吐场景的系统编程语言进入稳定阶段。Mojo 将 Python 式易用性与系统级性能结合，伴随逐步完善的工具链与库，旨在提升开发效率与运行效率。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49289512' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mcfunley.com/choose-boring-technology' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Choose Boring Technology (2015)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>选择无聊的技术（2015）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 10:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49289512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 423</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49289512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 241</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49289512'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues for choosing boring technology—reliable, boring options with long lifespans and easier maintenance over flashy, fad-driven stacks. It stresses simplicity, risk reduction, and sustainable engineering choices in real-world deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张在技术选型上优先选择“无聊但可靠”的方案，强调长期维护、稳定性和风险降低。通过对比示例，提出在真实部署中应避免盲目追逐热潮。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mcfunley.com/assets/images/mountains-square.jpg' data-full='https://mcfunley.com/assets/images/mountains-square.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sonic Pi 5 arrives with enhanced synthesis features and improved live-coding workflows, expanding its educational appeal and performance possibilities. The update strengthens its role as a playful, accessible tool for computer music.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49313428' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.severe-weather.eu/long-range-2/super-el-nino-growth-accelerating-to-record-strength-fall-winter-2026-2027-forecast-impact-united-states-canada-europe-fa/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Super El Niño Keeps Growing as New Forecasts Reach Record Territory Ahead Winter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>超级厄尔尼诺继续增强，预报达到创纪录水平</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 15, 2026 / 12:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49313428' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 416</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49313428' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 317</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49313428'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Super El Niño forecasts show growing intensity with record-setting projections ahead of winter, signaling potential heatwaves, droughts, and disruptions across North America and Europe.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>预测表明超级厄尔尼诺强度持续增强，冬季前达到创纪录水平，可能带来热浪、干旱及区域性气候干扰，波及北美和欧洲。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://www.severe-weather.eu/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/weather-fall-winter-2026-2027-forecast-united-states-canada-jet-stream-pressure-system-anomaly-temperature-snowfall-record-super-el-nino-event-unfolding-growing-faster-august-update.png' data-full='http://www.severe-weather.eu/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/weather-fall-winter-2026-2027-forecast-united-states-canada-jet-stream-pressure-system-anomaly-temperature-snowfall-record-super-el-nino-event-unfolding-growing-faster-august-update.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
