<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stowaway.live/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stowaway – Take the window seat on any plane or satellite overhead</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Stowaway：在任意航班抢靠窗座位并查看卫星的工具</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 07, 2026 / 06:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 411</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49209918'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stowaway is a tool that helps users secure a preferred window seat on flights and provides visibility into satellites overhead, blending travel convenience with space awareness. It demonstrates how real time data and simple UIs can improve trip planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Stowaway 是一个工具，帮助用户在航班中抢到靠窗座位，同时提供上空卫星轨迹的可视性，展示实时数据与简洁界面的应用潜力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49284774' data-tags='Finance,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49284774' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tradersunion.com/news/central-banks/show/2973571-deutsche-bank-becomes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Deutsche Bank becomes first foreign yuan clearing bank in Europe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德意志银行成为欧洲首家清算人民币的境外银行</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 05:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49284774' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 410</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49284774' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 469</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49284774'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Deutsche Bank has become the first foreign bank to clear yuan in Europe, enabling yuan settlement through European clearing networks. The move advances yuan internationalization beyond Asia and could attract more Chinese capital flows and cross-border trade in Europe. It also signals growing competition among European hubs to attract offshore renminbi activity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德意志银行成为欧洲首家清算人民币的境外银行，开启在欧元区进行人民币清算的服务。这一举动推动人民币在欧洲的国际化进程，或吸引更多人民币资金流向和跨境交易，并影响欧洲清算市场的格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tradersunion.com/uploads/images/tu-news/02026/08/2973571/deutsche-bank-becomes.jpg' data-full='https://tradersunion.com/uploads/images/tu-news/02026/08/2973571/deutsche-bank-becomes.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49321717' data-tags='Hardware,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49321717' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rvembedded.com/blog_post/12/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 3rd World Embedded Engineer Responds to &quot;RISC-V They Should Have Known Better&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>来自发展中国家的嵌入式工程师回应：RISC-V 他们本应更懂得</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 10:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49321717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 392</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49321717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 209</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49321717'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A developer from a developing region defends RISC-V against claims it is impractical. They emphasize openness, lower hardware costs, and resilient ecosystems for regional players, while acknowledging tooling gaps and maturity issues, arguing the real-world benefits outweigh the drawbacks for cost-sensitive devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>来自发展中国家的嵌入式工程师为 RISC-V 辩护，强调开放性、较低成本和本地生态对区域硬件制造的帮助，并承认工具链和生态成熟度的不足，但反驳其天生劣势观点。对于需要成本敏感设备的现实场景，RISC-V 的优势在于灵活性和供应链韧性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>聚焦在技术与生活中的丰富性，挑战匮乏思维，倡导更可持续、容错与协作友好的设计与实现方式。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49307592' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49307592' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://conic.al/writing/the-other-sean-byrne-doesnt-exist/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The other Sean Byrne doesn&#x27;t exist</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>另一位 Sean Byrne 不存在</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 21:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49307592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 381</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49307592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 182</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49307592'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay exploring identity and authorial authenticity, using the claim that &#x27;the other Sean Byrne doesn&#x27;t exist&#x27; to probe how online personas are constructed and how readers perceive who writes what.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇关于身份与署名真实性的随笔，借“另一位 Sean Byrne 不存在”之论点，考察网络身份的构建与读者如何认定作者。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nine PBS 因被限制访问档案数据而起诉 Iron Mountain</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 06:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49285418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 380</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49285418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 220</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49285418'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The broadcaster alleges that Iron Mountain blocked access to archival data, prompting legal action. The case highlights how vendor-controlled archives intersect with public-access requirements and preservation obligations. If the suit succeeds, it could influence data governance policies for libraries, broadcasters, and other institutions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该机构指控 Iron Mountain 阻断对档案数据的访问，提起诉讼。此案凸显了由厂商控制的档案与公开访问需求及保存义务之间的矛盾。若胜诉，可能影响图书馆、广播机构等组织的数据治理政策。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theconversation.com/taxi-drivers-rarely-die-of-alzheimers-how-complex-mental-maps-and-spatial-reasoning-protect-your-brain-286650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Taxi drivers rarely die of Alzheimer&#x27;s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>出租车司机罹患阿尔茨海默病的风险较低</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 08:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 379</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 276</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49232253'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study indicates taxi drivers&#x27; rich, complex spatial navigation and mental maps may protect against Alzheimer&#x27;s, highlighting how environmental cognitive training can influence brain resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示，出租车司机复杂的空间导航和地图记忆可能提升大脑对阿尔茨海默病的抵抗力，强调环境中的认知训练对脑部韧性的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.theconversation.com/files/752403/original/file-20260805-60-8x6hvk.jpg?ixlib=rb-4.1.1&amp;rect=0%2C161%2C2121%2C1060&amp;q=45&amp;auto=format&amp;w=1356&amp;h=668&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://images.theconversation.com/files/752403/original/file-20260805-60-8x6hvk.jpg?ixlib=rb-4.1.1&amp;rect=0%2C161%2C2121%2C1060&amp;q=45&amp;auto=format&amp;w=1356&amp;h=668&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.byhand.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI by Hand</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI by Hand</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 08:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49300568' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 359</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49300568' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49300568'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI by Hand explores human-in-the-loop AI, emphasizing how thoughtful human input can improve model alignment, safety, and practicality. The project highlights an approach where people guide or augment AI rather than replacing them.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI by Hand 探讨人机协作中的 AI，强调通过人为输入改善对齐、安全与实用性。该项目强调以人为主导的 AI 方案，而非单纯的全自动替代。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!uug6!,f_auto,q_auto:best,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Faibyhand.substack.com%2Ftwitter%2Fsubscribe-card.jpg%3Fv%3D-636237388%26version%3D9' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!uug6!,f_auto,q_auto:best,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Faibyhand.substack.com%2Ftwitter%2Fsubscribe-card.jpg%3Fv%3D-636237388%26version%3D9' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49267928' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://llama.app' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>llama.cpp</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>llama.cpp 项目</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 21:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49267928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 356</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49267928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 159</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49267928'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>llama.cpp is an open-source project that enables running Meta&#x27;s LLaMA models efficiently on CPU with minimal dependencies. It unlocks offline AI use cases and makes local inference accessible without GPUs, which matters for privacy-focused developers and hobbyists alike. It also broadens access for researchers and small teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>llama.cpp 是一个开源项目，使 LLaMA 模型能够在 CPU 上高效运行，依赖尽量精简。它为离线 AI 场景提供了可行性，便于在没有 GPU 的本地环境中推理，并扩大了研究者与小团队的接触面。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49298910' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49298910' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://e360.yale.edu/digest/australia-home-batteries' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>In Australia, a home battery boom has helped cut wholesale power prices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>澳大利亚家庭电池热潮帮助削减批发电价</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 07:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49298910' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 350</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49298910' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 307</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49298910'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Australia&#x27;s home battery boom is helping cut wholesale power prices by enabling households to store solar energy and shift demand. The trend underscores how distributed storage can ease grid stress and reshape energy economics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>澳大利亚家庭用电池热潮使家庭能够储存太阳能并错峰用电，从而压低批发电价。分布式储能对缓解电网压力、重塑能源经济具有重要意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://yale-threesixty.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/Home-Battery_NSWECA.jpg?w=1200&amp;q=60&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;dm=1786711355&amp;s=786f9b9062891e45a0bd1b54e7ef1a57' data-full='https://yale-threesixty.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/Home-Battery_NSWECA.jpg?w=1200&amp;q=60&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;dm=1786711355&amp;s=786f9b9062891e45a0bd1b54e7ef1a57' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49255710' data-tags='Business,Finance,AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49255710' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stratechery.com/2026/nvidias-risky-business/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia&#x27;s Risky Business</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英伟达的高风险商业策略</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 03:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49255710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 342</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49255710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 168</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49255710'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stratechery argues that Nvidia’s aggressive bets on AI compute demand, expanding software ecosystems, and potential regulatory scrutiny are high-stakes moves. The upside is market leadership, but margins could compress if demand softens or competitors erode the moat.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Stratechery 指出英伟达在 AI 计算需求、软件生态扩张以及潜在监管压力等方面的高风险布局。若需求减弱或竞争加剧，利润率可能承压；但也有通过成为 AI 基础设施核心而维持领先地位的潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s0.wp.com/_si/?t=eyJpbWciOiJodHRwczpcL1wvaTAud3AuY29tXC9zdHJhdGVjaGVyeS5jb21cL3dwLWNvbnRlbnRcL3VwbG9hZHNcLzIwMThcLzAzXC9jcm9wcGVkLWFuZHJvaWQtY2hyb21lLTUxMng1MTItMS5wbmc_Zml0PTUxMiUyQzUxMiZzc2w9MSIsInR4dCI6IlN0cmF0ZWNoZXJ5IGJ5IEJlbiBUaG9tcHNvbiIsInRlbXBsYXRlIjoiZWRnZSIsImZvbnQiOiIiLCJibG9nX2lkIjoxODgwNDM0MTV9.5VWck4PcKPWCTPe_HVznn3n3xsgn-G0b3d2OeiNNC7cMQ' data-full='https://s0.wp.com/_si/?t=eyJpbWciOiJodHRwczpcL1wvaTAud3AuY29tXC9zdHJhdGVjaGVyeS5jb21cL3dwLWNvbnRlbnRcL3VwbG9hZHNcLzIwMThcLzAzXC9jcm9wcGVkLWFuZHJvaWQtY2hyb21lLTUxMng1MTItMS5wbmc_Zml0PTUxMiUyQzUxMiZzc2w9MSIsInR4dCI6IlN0cmF0ZWNoZXJ5IGJ5IEJlbiBUaG9tcHNvbiIsInRlbXBsYXRlIjoiZWRnZSIsImZvbnQiOiIiLCJibG9nX2lkIjoxODgwNDM0MTV9.5VWck4PcKPWCTPe_HVznn3n3xsgn-G0b3d2OeiNNC7cMQ' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49249150' data-tags='Legal,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49249150' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://linuxstans.com/illinois-hb5511-operating-system-age-verification/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Illinois just passed a law that puts Linux on the hook for age verification</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊利诺伊州通过法案，将操作系统纳入年龄验证责任</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 13:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49249150' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 341</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49249150' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 511</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49249150'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Illinois passed a law that makes operating systems bear responsibility for age verification. This raises feasibility, enforcement, and open-source implications for Linux and other platforms as lawmakers seek to curb underage access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新法案要求操作系统具备并执行对受限内容的年龄验证，间接让 Linux 等操作系统承担相关义务。此举引发关于可行性、执法及对开源生态的影响等担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://linuxstans.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/illinois-hb5511-operating-system-age-verification.png' data-full='https://linuxstans.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/illinois-hb5511-operating-system-age-verification.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://x.ai/bot' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Grok Bot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Grok Bot</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49261514' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 339</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49261514' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 324</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49261514'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Grok Bot is an AI assistant from X.AI designed to handle emails, scheduling, and other productivity tasks with natural language understanding and proactive suggestions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Grok Bot 是 X.AI 推出的 AI 助手，能够通过自然语言理解处理电子邮件、日程等生产力任务，并提供主动建议。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49275385' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://artificialanalysis.ai/articles/grok-4-6-benchmarks-and-analysis' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Grok 4.6 scores 61 on the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Grok 4.6 在人工分析智能指数上得到61分</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 09:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49275385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 339</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49275385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 407</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49275385'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Grok 4.6 scores 61 on the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index, shedding light on where the model excels at analytical tasks and where it stumbles. The benchmark reveals strengths and limitations in reasoning reliability and error susceptibility, with implications for data-heavy workflows. It also prompts reflection on how well such indexes map to real-world problem solving in AI assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Grok 4.6 在人工分析智能指数上得到61分，揭示模型在分析任务中的强项与弱点。基准测试显示推理可靠性与对错误的易感性，为数据密集型工作流提供洞察，也引发关于此类指数与实际问题求解能力之间关系的思考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/6vfeftx9/articles/c65c69aedff721b918591059a76f55147f87d9ef-716x668.png?w=1200&amp;auto=format' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/6vfeftx9/articles/c65c69aedff721b918591059a76f55147f87d9ef-716x668.png?w=1200&amp;auto=format' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49300759' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49300759' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rustdesk.com/blog/unattended-remote-access-wayland/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RustDesk now supports true unattended remote access on Wayland</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RustDesk 在 Wayland 上实现真实的无监督远程访问</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 09:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49300759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 339</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49300759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49300759'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RustDesk adds true unattended remote access support on Wayland, enabling headless setups and smoother admin tasks on Linux desktops. This strengthens its suitability for remote maintenance while raising typical security considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RustDesk 在 Wayland 上增加了真正的无监督远程访问支持，适用于无头/远程运维的 Linux 桌面场景，同时需关注远程访问的安全性与权限控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://rustdesk.com/_astro/unattended-remote-access-wayland-og.BOcMxzVY_212cpk.webp' data-full='https://rustdesk.com/_astro/unattended-remote-access-wayland-og.BOcMxzVY_212cpk.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49272549' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49272549' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://guillaumetech.github.io/posts/jpg-scaling-chrome/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why tiny JPEGs look different in Chrome</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chrome 中小尺寸 JPEG 的显示差异原因</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 07:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49272549' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 333</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49272549' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49272549'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tiny JPEGs can render differently in Chrome due to the browser&#x27;s image downscaling and color subsampling behavior. Chrome&#x27;s decoding and scaling pipeline, along with DPR and subpixel rendering, can yield color shifts or sharpness changes compared with other browsers. Understanding this helps designers make consistent thumbnails and image previews.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome 在对小尺寸 JPEG 进行缩放时的解码与渲染策略会导致呈现与其他浏览器略有差异。浏览器的下采样、放大算法以及设备像素比等因素可能带来颜色偏差或清晰度变化。了解原因有助于设计师在缩略图和图片预览时实现更一致的显示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://guillaumetech.github.io/_astro/compare.DJwphvWD_1VRb2.webp' data-full='https://guillaumetech.github.io/_astro/compare.DJwphvWD_1VRb2.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49314403' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49314403' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.acc.org/about-acc/press-releases/2026/08/11/14/59/abdominal-fat-predicts-heart-disease-risk-better-than-bmi' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Abdominal fat predicts heart disease risk better than BMI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>腹部脂肪比BMI更能预测心血管疾病风险</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 15, 2026 / 14:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49314403' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 310</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49314403' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 276</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49314403'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New evidence shows abdominal fat is a stronger predictor of heart disease risk than BMI, underscoring the importance of central adiposity measures. The finding could influence screening practices and public health messaging by shifting focus from weight alone to fat distribution. Clinicians may increasingly consider waist circumference or visceral fat assessments in risk estimation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新证据表明腹部脂肪对心血管疾病风险的预测力优于BMI，强调了腹部肥胖等中心性脂肪指标的重要性。这一发现可能影响筛查实践与公共健康信息的传播，将重点从体重单一指标转向脂肪分布。临床上可能越来越在风险评估中考虑腰围或内脏脂肪等指标。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://www.acc.org/-/media/9E7FA8BE7B44449AB9993CC9237EF455.png' data-full='http://www.acc.org/-/media/9E7FA8BE7B44449AB9993CC9237EF455.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49300800' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49300800' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49259339' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/trycua/cua/blob/main/blog/gpu-passthrough-macos-vms.md' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple Silicon and macOS VMs: Faster LLM Inference with llama.cpp</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Apple Silicon 与 macOS 虚拟机：借助 llama.cpp 实现更快的 LLM 推理</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 07:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49259339' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 304</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49259339' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49259339'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple Silicon machines running macOS VMs can use GPU passthrough to speed up LLM inference with llama.cpp. The setup shows how Macs can squeeze more AI throughput for local or private-cloud workflows, though it comes with VM stability and driver caveats. Practical tips and pitfalls accompany the approach for developers exploring on-device AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Apple Silicon 的 Mac 上，通过为 macOS 虚拟机启用 GPU 直通，可以用 llama.cpp 加速大语言模型推理。这种方案展示了在 Mac 上提升 AI 处理能力的可行性，便于实现本地化或私有云推理。实施中的现实挑战包括虚拟机稳定性、驱动兼容性以及功耗与散热等问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/cd831ac511183cbabd8d9359bbf0cdbd499329fb0257b497795ad7c264beec61/trycua/cua' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/cd831ac511183cbabd8d9359bbf0cdbd499329fb0257b497795ad7c264beec61/trycua/cua' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49322107' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49322107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: Cloudflare silently injects its analytics when you switch nameservers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tell HN: Cloudflare 在切换域名服务器时悄然注入分析代码</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 10:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49322107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 304</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49322107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49322107'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A post alleges Cloudflare injects its analytics code when you switch DNS, enabling traffic telemetry without explicit consent. The finding raises questions about privacy, transparency, and the trade-offs of using a privacy-focused CDN.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有人指称 Cloudflare 在你切换 DNS 时悄然注入分析代码，进行流量遥测而未征得明确同意。这一发现引发对隐私、透明度及使用隐私导向 CDNs 时权衡取舍的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49309451' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://allen.bargi.org/notes/working-with-ai-feels-like-leadership/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Working with AI feels more like leadership than coding</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>与 AI 共事更多是领导力，而非编码</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 15, 2026 / 03:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49309451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 303</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49309451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 191</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49309451'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues that AI alters day-to-day work from coding tasks to orchestration, decision making, and people leadership. Effective collaboration with AI requires new skills in alignment, governance, and cross-disciplinary communication.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为与 AI 的日常工作从编码转向协作、决策与人际领导。要高效与 AI 合作，需要在对齐、治理与跨学科沟通等方面具备新技能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://allen.bargi.org/notes/working-with-ai-feels-like-leadership/social-card.png' data-full='https://allen.bargi.org/notes/working-with-ai-feels-like-leadership/social-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49270040' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://woxi.ad-si.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Woxi - Open-source Mathematica / Wolfram Language reimplementation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Woxi：开源的 Mathematica / Wolfram Language 重实现</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 03:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49270040' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 302</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49270040' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49270040'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Woxi offers an open-source reimplementation of Mathematica/Wolfram Language aimed at researchers and educators who want an accessible, libre alternative. It provides a scripting and computation environment that mimics core Wolfram features and symbolic computation. The project could lower barriers for experimentation and education in computational math.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Woxi 提供开源实现，旨在为研究者与教育者提供一个可访问的替代 Mathematica/Wolfram Language 的计算环境，模仿核心符号计算与脚本能力。该项目可能降低在计算性数学教育和研究中的门槛，并扩展自由工具的使用场景。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49272569' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://knownagents.com/insights' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Someone is running mass vulnerability scans, spoofing AI bots like ClaudeBot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>有人在进行大规模漏洞扫描，伪装成 ClaudeBot 等 AI 机器人</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 07:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49272569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 301</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49272569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 226</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49272569'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Threat researchers report mass vulnerability scanning that spoofs AI bots like ClaudeBot to evade detection. The practice complicates attribution, strains rate limits, and can hamper legitimate AI services that rely on bot-like traffic patterns. For defenders, it underscores the need for robust fingerprinting and anomaly detection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全研究者发现有人在进行大规模漏洞扫描，并伪装成像 ClaudeBot 这样的 AI 机器人以逃避检测。此举增加了追责难度，可能挤占限流资源，并干扰依赖机器人流量模式的服务。对防御方而言，这凸显了加强指纹识别和异常流量检测的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://knownagents.com/assets/hero.png' data-full='https://knownagents.com/assets/hero.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chicken Scheme 6.0 发布，带来库的扩展、运行时性能改进和对现有 Scheme 生态的更好兼容。此举显示了轻量级 Scheme 编译器在嵌入和脚本场景中的持续活力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49232221' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49232221' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/08/09/historian-jill-lepore-says-the-tech-industry-is-led-by-bad-readers-who-are-undermining-democracy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Silicon Valley misreads science fiction and undermines democracy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>硅谷对科幻的误读正在削弱民主</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 08:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 294</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 267</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49232221'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jill Lepore argues Silicon Valley leaders are bad readers who misunderstand science fiction and its implications for democracy. The piece argues that tech culture&#x27;s techno-optimism and underappreciation of history undermine policy and accountability. For technologists and policymakers, it underscores the need for literary and historical literacy in tech leadership.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>吉尔·勒波尔指出硅谷领导者对科幻的解读严重偏离现实，忽视科幻对民主的潜在影响。文章批评科技文化的技术至上与历史认知不足可能削弱政策与问责。对技术人员与决策者而言，这强调提升文学与历史素养的重要性。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49231809' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49231809' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.w3.org/Provider/Style/URI' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cool URIs Don&#x27;t Change (1998)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>稳定的 URI 不会改变（1998）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 07:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49231809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 288</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49231809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49231809'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A foundational memo urging that URIs be stable, permanent identifiers for resources. It argues against design patterns that rely on mutable URLs, explaining how changing links breaks navigation, indexing, and caching—lessons that still influence modern web architecture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份1998年的基石性备忘录强调 URI 应保持稳定、永久指向资源，反对依赖可变链接的设计模式。链接变动会破坏导航、搜索索引与缓存，这些经验至今仍深刻影响现代 Web 架构。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.w3.org/Icons/WWW/w3c_home.gif' data-full='https://www.w3.org/Icons/WWW/w3c_home.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49310682' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/innovation/the-first-at-home-test-for-infected-ticks-could-improve-lyme-disease-diagnosis-180989235/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>At-home test for infected ticks could improve Lyme Disease diagnosis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>首个在家检测感染蜱虫的测试或提升莱姆病诊断</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 15, 2026 / 07:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49310682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 287</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49310682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49310682'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The first at-home test to identify infected ticks could shift Lyme disease diagnosis toward earlier, more accurate triage. If validated, it enables people to assess exposure risk before symptoms appear, though accuracy and interpretation will be key.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>首个在家检测感染蜱虫的测试可能将莱姆病诊断转向更早且更精确的分诊。若经验证，该测试将使人们在出现症状前就能评估暴露风险，但准确性和解读方式将成为关键。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/zHvX_MeCmgyz7Mfc3JnZBzfRUgU=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(1061x707:1062x708)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/44/ad/44ad8f8b-e8c6-4c3b-9d22-10eececf06c3/gettyimages-1602304607.jpg' data-full='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/zHvX_MeCmgyz7Mfc3JnZBzfRUgU=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(1061x707:1062x708)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/44/ad/44ad8f8b-e8c6-4c3b-9d22-10eececf06c3/gettyimages-1602304607.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Software engineering fundamentals matter more than ever as teams navigate AI tooling, distributed work, and complex codebases. The post argues for preserving core practices like clean design, testing, and robust versioning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>软件工程的基础原理在 AI、分布式工作与复杂代码库的时代显得更加重要，文章呼吁坚持测试、设计与版本管理等核心实践。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://claude.com/blog/auto-mode-default-in-claude-code' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Auto mode is now the default in Claude Code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 的自动模式现已设为默认</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 20:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49239021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 283</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49239021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 308</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49239021'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code now defaults to auto mode, speeding up coding workflows by automatically drafting suggestions and actions. Users can still adjust behavior, but the change reduces friction for everyday tasks and highlights how proactive AI assistants are becoming. This shift may influence how teams design coding sessions and reviews.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 的自动模式现已设为默认，从而加速编码工作流，自动给出建议与动作。用户仍可调整设置，但这一改动降低了日常任务的门槛，凸显了 AI 助手日益主动的趋势，并可能影响团队的编码工作流程与评审方式。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://w4g1.dev/blog/models-are-getting-dumber-on-purpose' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Models Are Getting Dumber on Purpose</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>模型在有意降级以提升安全性</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 12:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49322695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 279</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49322695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49322695'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that AI models are getting intentionally dumber to improve safety, control, or cost, rather than simply getting smarter from more data. It discusses the tension between capability and governance, and what that means for developers relying on ever more powerful systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为，AI 模型正被有意降级，以提高安全性、可控性以及成本效益，而不仅仅是靠更多数据来提升能力。讨论了能力与治理之间的张力，以及开发者在日益强大的系统面前所面临的取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://w4g1.dev/avatar.webp' data-full='https://w4g1.dev/avatar.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49232110' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techeblog.com/tom-stanton-supersonic-trebuchet/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tom Stanton&#x27;s supersonic trebuchet breaks sound barrier with gravity alone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>汤姆·斯坦顿的超音速投石机仅靠重力突破声障</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 08:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 276</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 103</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49232110'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tom Stanton&#x27;s gravity-powered trebuchet achieves supersonic release speeds, beating the sound barrier using only gravitational potential energy. It&#x27;s a clever demonstration of physics and energy transfer at macro scales.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>汤姆·斯坦顿的重力驱动投石机在仅靠重力势能的情况下实现超音速发射，突破音速。这个演示展示了宏观尺度的能量储存与动量传递的物理原理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.techeblog.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/09001255/tom-stanton-supersonic-trebuchet.jpg' data-full='https://images.techeblog.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/09001255/tom-stanton-supersonic-trebuchet.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49218040' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49218040' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-08-qa-mushroom-tiny-people-hallucinations.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mushroom behind &#x27;tiny people&#x27; hallucinations identified</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>识别出引发小人幻觉的蘑菇</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 07, 2026 / 18:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49218040' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49218040' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 267</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49218040'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers have identified the mushroom species behind reports of tiny people hallucinations during certain psychedelic experiences. The finding sheds light on how specific compounds reshape visual perception, with implications for safety, medical research, and foragers&#x27; awareness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员已确定在某些迷幻体验中引发小人幻觉的蘑菇种类。该发现揭示了特定成分如何改变视觉感知，为安全使用、药物研究和野外采集者的认知提高了理解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/mushroom-behind-tiny-p.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/mushroom-behind-tiny-p.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49279928' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49279928' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://okmij.org/ftp/Computation/Impressions/PrincipiaMathematica.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Principia Mathematica is modern and insightful</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《Principia Mathematica》现代且富有洞见</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 16:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49279928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 272</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49279928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49279928'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Principia Mathematica laid the rigorous groundwork for modern logic and foundational mathematics. Its ideas underpin contemporary automated reasoning, formal verification, and type systems in programming languages, and its layered approach to axioms and inference still informs how we build reliable software today.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Principia Mathematica》奠定了现代逻辑与基础数学的坚实框架，其内容在自动推理、形式化验证和编程语言的类型系统中仍具影响力。其对公理与推理的层次化思路，至今为构建可靠软件提供了宝贵的借鉴。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49289465' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49289465' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://donkeybas.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Donkey.bas is 45 Years Old – 131 line of Glory</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Donkey.bas 的 45 年纪念聚焦于这段131行的 BASIC 小程序如何成为极简主义与持续影响力的典范。它揭示微小代码也能激发现代创作与学习灵感。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://duckdb.org/2026/07/31/asynchronous-io' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Asynchronous I/O in DuckDB: Work, Thread, Work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DuckDB 的异步 I/O：工作、线程、工作</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 06:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49243061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 272</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49243061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49243061'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DuckDB adds asynchronous I/O, enabling query processing to overlap with I/O operations via background workers. This reduces stalls on large scans and improves throughput for analytics workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DuckDB 引入异步 I/O，使查询处理能与磁盘操作并行执行，借助后台工作线程降低等待，提高对大规模分析的吞吐量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://duckdb.org/images/blog/thumbs/async.jpg' data-full='https://duckdb.org/images/blog/thumbs/async.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/riemann-zeta' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Learning more about Claude&#x27;s mathematical capabilities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>进一步了解 Claude 的数学能力</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 10:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49247070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 270</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49247070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49247070'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic researchers evaluate Claude&#x27;s mathematical reasoning, testing symbolic tasks and number theory to gauge its capabilities and limitations. The findings show clear progress in some areas but also persistent gaps in formal proofs and complex computations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 的研究团队评估 Claude 的数学推理能力，测试符号推理与数论等任务，旨在了解其能力边界。结果显示在某些领域取得明显进步，但在证明与复杂计算方面仍存在不足。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/ca772f1b34e5b58e38694538f017d63f5dd196d9-1200x630.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/ca772f1b34e5b58e38694538f017d63f5dd196d9-1200x630.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://photosynthesis.camera' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: iPhone app takes simultaneous images from 2 lenses, fuses into 1 photo</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：iPhone 应用实现两镜头同时拍摄并融合成一张照片</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 08, 2026 / 15:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49226623' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 266</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49226623' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49226623'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A mobile app captures frames from both iPhone lenses in parallel and fuses them into a single image. This approach demonstrates how dual-camera data can be combined to improve resolution, detail, or dynamic range, pushing the capabilities of mobile photography. It hints at broader potential for real-time computational photography on consumer devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款移动应用并行从两枚 iPhone 镜头获取帧并融合成一张高质量照片。通过融合双镜头数据来提升分辨率、细节和动态范围，展示了计算摄影在手机上的潜力。这类技术为在日常设备上实现更强大摄影能力提供了可行路径。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49310926' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49310926' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dampfkraft.com/ghost-characters.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A spectre is haunting Unicode</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Unicode 遇到幽灵字符</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 15, 2026 / 07:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49310926' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 266</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49310926' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49310926'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Unicode’s complexity allows visually similar or confusable characters to cause spoofing and security risks. The piece cites concrete examples and argues for stricter normalization and rendering controls to reduce abuse in critical contexts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Unicode 的复杂性使视觉上相似或易混淆的字符可能被利用进行伪装，带来安全风险。文中给出具体示例，并主张在规范化和渲染控制方面加强措施，以在关键场景中降低滥用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dampfkraft.com/by-id/a824aa10/img/ghostcanvas.l.jpg' data-full='https://dampfkraft.com/by-id/a824aa10/img/ghostcanvas.l.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49261218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 261</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49261218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 240</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49261218'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A woman says a Flock surveillance alert linked her to a homicide, leading to two police stops. The incident highlights the risk of misidentification in automated policing tools and the real-world consequences for civilians.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名女性称，Flock 监控系统的警报将她与一起凶杀案相关联，导致她两次被警方拦下。这一事件凸显了自动化警务工具在误识别方面的风险，以及对平民造成的现实后果。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49255672'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>More than ten firms pay up to $100,000 a month for access to Truth Social posts to monitor sentiment and trends. This reveals opaque data-access practices on fringe networks and raises privacy and ethics questions for brands and researchers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>超过十家企业为获取 Truth Social 帖子访问权，月费高达十万美元，以监控情感与趋势。这一做法揭示了小众网络的数据访问模式的隐蔽性，并对品牌与研究机构的隐私与伦理提出质疑。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49294199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 260</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49294199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blogs.nvidia.com/blog/nemotron-lightning-switchyard-rtx-dgx/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia Nemotron 3.5 Lightning and NeMo Switchyard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英伟达 Nemotron 3.5 Lightning 与 NeMo Switchyard</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 12:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49263340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 259</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49263340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 135</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49263340'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nvidia expands Nemotron 3.5 with Lightning and introduces NeMo Switchyard to improve deployment and orchestration for AI workloads across DGX clusters. The update emphasizes streamlined model serving, unified management, and higher throughput for large-scale inference. For enterprises building AI-powered apps, it suggests a more integrated, scalable path from model development to production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英伟达推出 Nemotron 3.5 的 Lightning 版本并引入 NeMo Switchyard，优化在 DGX 集群上的 AI 部署与编排。升级聚焦统一管理、提升推理吞吐，帮助企业将模型从开发到生产的落地更高效。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogs.nvidia.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/image1.jpeg' data-full='https://blogs.nvidia.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/image1.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49270022' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gowers.wordpress.com/2026/08/12/what-sort-of-maths-are-llms-good-at/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What sort of maths are LLMs good at?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型语言模型擅长哪些数学领域？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 03:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49270022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 258</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49270022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 150</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49270022'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LLMs show proficiency in certain math domains, especially structured, rule-based tasks, but struggle with novel proofs and deep abstraction. The discussion maps which areas align with training data and inference patterns, and why prompting and tooling matter for boosting performance. It helps readers decide when to rely on LLMs for math versus formal proof systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大型语言模型在某些数学领域表现出色，尤其是结构化、规则性强的任务，但在新颖证明和深层抽象方面存在短板。讨论梳理了哪些数学领域与训练数据及推理模式更契合，以及为何通过提示与工具能提升表现。帮助读者判断在数学任务中应如何在依赖 LLM 与使用正式证明系统之间取舍。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49290215' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49290215' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/systemd/systemd/issues/40262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Single log line is 49KB+ (ext4) / 110KB+ (btrfs) of systemd-journald disk writes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>systemd 日志单行在 ext4/btrfs 下的极大写入</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 11:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49290215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 254</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49290215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 219</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49290215'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Notes that a single systemd-journald log line can trigger very large disk writes on ext4 and btrfs. This has implications for log throughput, storage planning, and how journaling backends should handle extreme entries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出 systemd-journald 的单条日志行在 ext4 下会触发极大的磁盘写入，在 btrfs 下也如此。这对日志吞吐、存储规划以及日志后端处理极端日志条目提出了考虑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e27ba23c438b779ffd11faa49a642375743a98e6c15ad152d3a5694f7c1e0d64/systemd/systemd/issues/40262' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e27ba23c438b779ffd11faa49a642375743a98e6c15ad152d3a5694f7c1e0d64/systemd/systemd/issues/40262' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vectoral.com/blog/who-are-the-token-brokers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The AI Credit Resale Economy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 计算信用再销售经济</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 07:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49320611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 235</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49320611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49320611'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An emerging market is forming around trading AI compute credits and usage rights, with token brokers providing liquidity and signaling prices. The system could lower the barrier to AI access for startups and individuals, but it also invites resale scams and regulatory scrutiny as standards and protections emerge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个围绕AI计算信用和使用权买卖的新兴市场正在形成，经纪人聚合流动性并传递价格信号。该模式可能降低创业公司和个人获得AI能力的门槛，但随着标准化和保护机制尚未完善，也引发转卖欺诈与监管关注。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49300671'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>France&#x27;s top court blocked a ban on social media for under-15s, ruling it infringes freedom of expression. The decision frames debates about age-based access, digital rights, and how to protect minors online.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国最高法院裁定针对未满15岁用户的社交媒体禁令侵犯表达自由，推翻相关规定。这一裁决引发关于未成年在线保护和数字权利的辩论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ethw.org/Milestones:Parametron,_1954' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Parametron: 50s Japanese computer that uses neither transistors nor vacuum tubes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Parametron：不使用晶体管或真空管的50年代日本计算机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 03:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49241846' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 230</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49241846' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49241846'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Parametron was a 1950s Japanese computer that used parametric oscillators instead of transistors or vacuum tubes. It illustrates an alternative digital-logic approach that helped launch Japan’s early computing era before transistorization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Parametron 是一台1950年代的日本计算机，使用参数振荡器来实现逻辑门，不依赖晶体管或真空管。它展示了在晶体管普及前，不同于传统电子元件的数字逻辑路径，并推动了日本早期计算的发展。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reader asked for their data from McDonald’s loyalty program and discovered a 515-page dossier generated from their activity, preferences, and location data. The depth of profiling raises privacy and data-retention concerns about loyalty programs and how this data might be used. It also highlights questions about data portability and consent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位用户申请了麦当劳忠诚计划的数据副本，发现系统建立了包含活动、偏好和位置历史在内的约515页档案。此举凸显现代忠诚计划对个人数据的深度采集，引发隐私、数据留存与可移植性方面的担忧。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The CFTC has invoked emergency authority to ensure Kalshi can keep operating in New York amid market disruptions. The move underscores how regulators manage access to emerging event-based trading platforms while preserving market integrity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国商品期货交易委员会宣布在市场混乱时行使紧急权力，确保 Kalshi 能在纽约继续运营。此举凸显监管机构在新兴事件驱动交易平台的准入与市场完整性之间的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49249523' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49249523' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://softwaredoug.com/blog/2026/08/10/hypothetical-classifications' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t classify, hallucinate</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>别再做分类，别让模型产生幻觉！</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 13:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49249523' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 221</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49249523' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49249523'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that forcing ML models into rigid classification tasks can degrade usefulness and invites embracing generative approaches with guardrails. It highlights practical strategies to reduce hallucinations, including prompt design, transparency, and testing against real-world distributions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中强调将模型置于僵硬的分类任务容易放大错误，鼓励在某些场景采用生成性方法并设置防错边界。提出通过提示设计、结果透明、基于实际分布的测试等手段来降低幻觉风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://softwaredoug.com/assets/media/dougie_in_snuggie_social.jpg' data-full='https://softwaredoug.com/assets/media/dougie_in_snuggie_social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49247477' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49247477' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.vectorware.com/blog/simd-on-gpu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rust SIMD on the GPU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 在 GPU 上的 SIMD</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 11:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49247477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 218</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49247477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49247477'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rust SIMD on the GPU analyzes how to map Rust’s SIMD-style parallelism to GPU architectures to boost throughput for numeric kernels. It highlights memory transfer, safety, and tooling challenges when crossing CPU-RAM to GPU. Takeaway: offloading compute-heavy Rust workloads to GPUs can yield big speedups, but it demands careful data layout and tool support.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rust 的 SIMD 在 GPU 上的实现探讨将 Rust 的向量并行性映射到 GPU 架构，以提升数值计算的吞吐量。文章指出在 CPU 与显存/显卡之间的数据传输、内存管理与安全性方面的挑战，以及现有工具的不足。要点是：将重量级 Rust 计算下沉到 GPU 可以带来显著加速，但需要精心的数据布局和工具链支持。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.vectorware.com/_next/image/?url=%2F_next%2Fstatic%2Fmedia%2Fvectorware_logo.6d5f5210.png&amp;w=640&amp;q=75&amp;dpl=dpl_4ZmndNCZY2uSnEayhwbSuemEqYJP' data-full='https://www.vectorware.com/_next/image/?url=%2F_next%2Fstatic%2Fmedia%2Fvectorware_logo.6d5f5210.png&amp;w=640&amp;q=75&amp;dpl=dpl_4ZmndNCZY2uSnEayhwbSuemEqYJP' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN project where you conduct interviews with AI suspects using only your voice, turning a classic mystery into an interactive, voice-controlled experience. The project showcases the potential of natural-language interfaces and real-time AI dialogue, with entertainment value and design trade-offs around privacy and trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN：通过语音与 AI 嫌疑人对话的互动推理游戏，将传统谜案转化为语音驱动的体验。该项目展示了自然语言界面和实时 AI 对话的潜力，兼具娱乐性与对隐私、信任的设计权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pubsonline.informs.org/doi/full/10.1287/orsc.2024.19981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Analyzing data from Silicon Valley ventures and founders prosecuted for fraud</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>分析来自硅谷风险投资与被控欺诈的创始人数据</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 08:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232318' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 197</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232318' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49232318'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An academic study analyzes data on Silicon Valley ventures and founders prosecuted for fraud to identify patterns and risk factors. The findings point to governance gaps, over-optimism, and misaligned incentives that can enable fraud, underscoring the need for stronger due diligence and oversight in venture funding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该研究分析硅谷风险投资与因欺诈被起诉的创始人数据，揭示治理缺口、过度乐观与激励错位等风险因素。研究强调需要更严格的尽职调查与监管，以抑制欺诈行为并提升投资透明度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/company-offering-100-human-written-never-ai-peer-review-is-entirely-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Company Offering &#x27;100% Human-Written, Never AI&#x27; Medical Research Is 100% AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>声称‘100% 人工撰写、从不使用 AI’ 的医学研究公司 实际上 全部由 AI 生成</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 19:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49267057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49267057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49267057'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A company claiming to publish &#x27;100% human-written, never AI&#x27; medical research is revealed to be entirely AI-generated. The revelation underscores risks of misleading scientific communication and pressures on peer review.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家公司声称提供‘100% 人工撰写、从不使用 AI’ 的医学研究，但事实却是全部由 AI 生成。这一发现突显了科学传播中的误导风险，以及对同行评审体系的压力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.neowin.net/news/facebook-ads-are-so-hard-to-block-that-ublock-origin-stopped-filtering-them/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Facebook ads are so hard to block that uBlock Origin stopped filtering them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Facebook 广告难以拦截，uBlock Origin 停止过滤</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 05:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49271126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49271126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49271126'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Facebook ads have become so hard to block that a popular ad blocker stopped filtering them, illustrating an ongoing arms race between platform monetization and ad-blocking tools. The episode highlights the challenges of keeping users from unwanted ads and the limits of browser-based privacy tools. It invites questions about user choice, transparency, and blocking effectiveness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Facebook 广告变得难以拦截，知名广告拦截工具甚至停止了对其过滤，凸显了平台变现策略与广告拦截工具之间的持续博弈。这一事件凸显了让用户屏蔽广告的难度以及浏览器隐私工具的局限性，也引发关于用户选择、透明度与拦截效果的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon backs a fossil-fueled power plant that could become the top source of climate pollution in the U.S., potentially conflicting with its climate pledges. The choice spotlights tensions between reliability, economics, and environmental commitments in big-tech supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亚马逊资助的燃气发电站可能成为美国最大的气候污染源，似乎与公司自述的气候承诺相冲突。这一举措凸显供应链弹性、能源经济与环保承诺之间的矛盾，或将影响相关政策和能源投资议题。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A dataset of 50,000 boat names offers a ready-made resource for NLP, branding tests, and string-processing experiments. It also reveals cultural quirks in naming and can serve as a testbed for name-generation and anomaly detection.</p>
