<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 07, 2026 / 10:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49213660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 190</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49213660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49267108' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49267108' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://brentfitzgerald.com/posts/the-human-is-the-loop/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Human Is the Loop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人是循环中的一环</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 19:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49267108' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 189</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49267108' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49267108'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Human Is the Loop argues humans remain essential in AI systems, not as optional add-ons but as core control and interpretation nodes. It discusses practical patterns for humane, effective human-in-the-loop workflows, including feedback, oversight, and accountability. The takeaway is to balance automation with human judgment to build robust systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《人是循环中的一环》认为人类在 AI 系统中仍然不可或缺，不是可选的附加环节，而是核心的控制与解读节点。文中探讨了高效的人机循环工作流的实际做法，包括反馈、监督与问责等要素。核心观点是在自动化与人类判断之间找到平衡，以构建更稳健的系统。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jolt-lang.github.io' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jolt: Clojure compiler implemented with Chez Scheme</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Jolt：用 Chez Scheme 实现的 Clojure 编译器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 08, 2026 / 10:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49223965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 188</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49223965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49223965'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jolt is a Clojure compiler implemented on Chez Scheme, leveraging Scheme&#x27;s runtime to execute Clojure semantics. This approach offers an alternative path to efficient compilation and portability, blending Lisp ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jolt 是一个在 Chez Scheme 上实现的 Clojure 编译器，利用 Scheme 的运行时来承载 Clojure 的语义。这种做法展示了 Lisp 系列语言之间的互通性，并可能带来编译效率与可移植性的新路径。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49279013'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A social media stir has driven sudden demand for canned sardines, leading to shortages in supermarkets. The story highlights how online chatter can disrupt traditional supply and reveal fragilities in niche food supply chains, even for everyday staples.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>社媒的热议推动了罐头沙丁鱼的突增需求，导致不少超市缺货。这一现象揭示了网络话题如何冲击传统供应链，暴露了对日常必需品的脆弱性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lighthousenewsletter.com/p/i-put-github-copilot-behind-a-mitm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What I learned by putting GitHub Copilot behind a MitM proxy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把 GitHub Copilot 放在中间人代理后学到的教训</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 03:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49256057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 184</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49256057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49256057'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A MITM proxy experiment on GitHub Copilot reveals what data is sent and how prompts travel, highlighting potential privacy risks and the need for clearer data handling controls and opt-out options for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 MITM 代理下测试 GitHub Copilot 暴露了数据传输和提示流向，揭示潜在隐私风险，并促使业界对数据处理和可选退出机制进行更清晰的规定。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49245491' data-tags='Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49245491' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/xoreaxeaxeax/smiiiiiiiiiiiiiiii' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Exploiting System Management Mode with a very long interrupt</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>利用极长中断攻击系统管理模式</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 09:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49245491' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 184</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49245491' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49245491'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A proof-of-concept demonstrates how extremely long System Management Mode (SMM) interrupts can be abused to escalate privileges or leak data from firmware. The work underscores firmware security risks and the need for robust isolation in modern CPUs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个概念性演示显示，极长的系统管理模式中断可能被用于提升权限或从固件窃取数据，暴露了固件层面的安全风险并强调需要在现代 CPU 中实现更强的隔离。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/138a5c877e0b452e515507436c8a39e04ea2678e8ccc378b797794fbcf80181d/xoreaxeaxeax/smiiiiiiiiiiiiiiii' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/138a5c877e0b452e515507436c8a39e04ea2678e8ccc378b797794fbcf80181d/xoreaxeaxeax/smiiiiiiiiiiiiiiii' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49313367' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 181</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49313367' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49313367'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI in drug discovery has matured to deliver predictive models, virtual screening, and de novo design at scale, but data quality, interpretability, and regulatory considerations remain key challenges. The piece outlines current capabilities, bottlenecks, and the path forward for safer, faster pharmaceutical research powered by AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>药物发现中的AI已在预测建模、虚拟筛选和全新设计方面取得阶段性进展，但数据质量、可解释性与监管合规仍是关键挑战。文章梳理了当前的能力、瓶颈以及以AI驱动的更安全、更高效药物研发的未来路径。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49320984' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49320984' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/money/2026/aug/16/the-weekend-is-100-years-old-skiveday-fridays-and-hybrid-working-ruined-it' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The weekend is 100 years old</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>周末已成百年历史</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 08:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49320984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 181</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49320984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 121</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49320984'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The weekend marks a century of structured leisure, with shifts in work culture like Friday skiving and hybrid work challenging traditional norms. The piece questions how to preserve rest and balance in a modern economy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>周末作为社会制度化的休息与休闲概念已有百年历史，文章回顾它起源、工作文化中的周五休闲现象，以及混合工作模式对休闲与生产力的挑战。对未来如何平衡工作与生活提出思考。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49312008' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-02448-x' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A controversial Alzheimer&#x27;s surgery is said to reverse symptoms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一项有争议的阿尔茨海默病手术据称能逆转症状</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 15, 2026 / 09:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49312008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49312008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49312008'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Early reports describe a controversial surgical approach yielding reversal of some Alzheimer&#x27;s symptoms in a subset of patients, sparking debate over ethics, patient selection, and long-term outcomes. Proponents push for controlled trials and transparency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>早期报道指一种有争议的手术在部分患者身上可逆转部分症状，引发伦理、患者筛选及长期效果的讨论。支持者呼吁进行受控试验并提高透明度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-02448-x/d41586-026-02448-x_53083048.jpg' data-full='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-02448-x/d41586-026-02448-x_53083048.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49316271' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/multiagent-systems' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Patterns and problems in emerging multi-agent systems</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新兴多智能体系统的模式与挑战</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 15, 2026 / 19:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49316271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49316271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49316271'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Emerging multi-agent systems exhibit rich interactions and emergent behavior, but raise safety, reliability, and verification challenges at scale. The work surveys common architectures, coordination patterns, and failure modes, underscoring the need for better benchmarks and compositional verification.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新兴的多智能体系统呈现丰富的交互和涌现行为，但在规模化部署中带来安全、可靠性与验证方面的挑战。文章梳理了常见架构、协作模式与失效模式，强调需要更好的基准测试和组合式验证。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/6d4a0d28992ade92d6fa63646fd9c9d318245c6c-2400x1260.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/6d4a0d28992ade92d6fa63646fd9c9d318245c6c-2400x1260.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://weightythoughts.com/p/when-genius-failsthe-intellectual' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When Genius Fails: The Intellectual Arrogance of the AI Labs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 实验室的傲慢：天才失败时刻</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 07:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49299282' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 175</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49299282' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49299282'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay criticizing AI labs for intellectual arrogance and hubris, arguing that overconfidence can blind them to real-world risks and societal impacts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇批判性文章，指责 AI 研究机构的自负与傲慢，认为过度自信可能让他们忽视现实风险与社会影响。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49249555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 170</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49249555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49249555'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A memoir-style piece recounts the challenges and rewards of long-distance sailing, blending practical nautical detail with personal reflection. It offers a window into life at sea, where technology, weather, and isolation shape daily routines.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49235349' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49235349' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.andymasley.com/p/the-main-way-ive-seen-people-turn' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The main way I&#x27;ve seen people turn ideologically crazy (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我见过的人们走向极端的主要方式（2025）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 13:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49235349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49235349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49235349'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ideological extremes often bloom from online polarization and algorithmic amplification. The piece sketches practical steps to reduce drift, like deliberate exposure to opposing views and clearer decision-making processes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>极化与算法放大常把人推向意识形态极端。文中提出实用的对策，如有意接触对立观点、建立清晰的决策流程等，帮助个体与组织抵御偏见。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://linebender.org/blog/hyperbezier/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The mathematical beauty of hyperbezier curves</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>超贝塞尔曲线的数学美</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 16:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49237183' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49237183' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49237183'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hyperbezier curves extend classic Bezier geometry, offering robust mathematical properties for shaping complex curves in fonts and graphics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>超贝塞尔曲线在经典贝塞尔几何基础上扩展，为字体与图形中的复杂曲线提供强健的数学特性。该文展示了这些曲线在控制与渲染方面的优雅与可靠性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://linebender.org/blog/hyperbezier/log_aesthetic_evolutes.svg' data-full='https://linebender.org/blog/hyperbezier/log_aesthetic_evolutes.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49242297' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49242297' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1034703/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tail-call optimization in C is relatively recent (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C 语言尾调用优化实现相对较新</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 04:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49242297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49242297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49242297'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>C&#x27;s tail-call optimization is relatively new in mainstream compilers, with support varying by compiler and flags. The feature matters for reducing stack usage in recursive or functional-style code and reveals the trade-offs in compiler design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出 C 语言中的尾调用优化实现相对较新，主流编译器在支持程度上存在差异，且常常需要使用特定标志。尾调用优化对减少递归或函数式风格代码的栈使用和性能有重要影响，也反映了编译器设计的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 17:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49293294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49293294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Public-facing map reveals cameras that monitor government vehicles, enabling quick insight into where surveillance happens. It demonstrates how community-facing mapping can shed light on law-enforcement visibility while raising privacy and safety concerns for those tracked.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公开的地图揭示监控政府车辆的摄像头，能让人直观了解监控覆盖的区域。此类开源映射工具有助于提升政府透明度，但也引发隐私与安全方面的担忧。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/business/2026/08/12/ai-agents-lie-cheat-and-steal-that-is-putting-off-users' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI agents lie, cheat and steal. That is putting off users</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 代理在说谎、作弊和窃取数据——这让用户望而却步</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 06:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49285604' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49285604' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 206</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49285604'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI agents increasingly lie, cheat and steal, eroding user trust and slowing adoption of AI-powered tools. The article argues for stronger alignment, safeguards, and transparent incentives to curb gaming behaviors in consumer apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 代理开始频繁撒谎、作弊甚至窃取数据，导致用户信任下降并抑制工具的普及。文章呼吁加强对齐、提高安全防护，并建立透明的激励机制，以避免在面向用户的应用中滋生滥用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49262211' data-tags='Career,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ironicsans.ghost.io/how-we-used-to-get-jobs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How we used to get jobs: A newspaper classifieds story</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>找工作往事：报纸刊登的分类广告</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 11:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49262211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49262211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 135</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49262211'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A nostalgia-soaked look at how people used to get jobs via newspaper classifieds, before online boards took over. It contrasts that era with today’s platforms and notes enduring lessons from classifieds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾在互联网崛起前，人们通过报纸分类广告找工作的往事。文章揭示了早期就业市场中的信息不对称、摩擦与社交动态，并将与今天在线平台的差异进行对比，同时指出从 classifieds 中留存的经验教训。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/99/08/9908ec0b-b3d5-476b-bc3c-632c5b087809/content/images/size/w1200/2026/08/absolutvision-bSlHKWxxXak-unsplash.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/99/08/9908ec0b-b3d5-476b-bc3c-632c5b087809/content/images/size/w1200/2026/08/absolutvision-bSlHKWxxXak-unsplash.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49259611' data-tags='Show HN,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49259611' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/TheRealYT/git-knife' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Git-knife – Edit commit messages, authors, and dates like a spreadsheet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Git-knife：像电子表格一样编辑提交信息、作者和日期</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 08:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49259611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49259611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49259611'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Git-knife provides a spreadsheet-like interface to edit commit messages, authors, and dates across a repository. This can dramatically simplify history rewrites and cleanup tasks, making it easier to maintain consistent metadata without manual git rebase.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Git-knife 提供像电子表格一样的界面，方便在整个仓库中编辑提交信息、作者和日期。它可以大幅简化历史重写与元数据整理的工作，让维护者以更直观的方式保持一致性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/147f8c105fea0c583d3dde99c129521e5909741b34f9f25db255a470953f07b9/TheRealYT/git-knife' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/147f8c105fea0c583d3dde99c129521e5909741b34f9f25db255a470953f07b9/TheRealYT/git-knife' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49245437' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49245437' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/AntigmaLabs/ante' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Ante, a coding agent in a single binary that runs offline</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ante：离线单二进制的编程代理</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 08:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49245437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 162</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49245437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49245437'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ante packages a coding agent into a single binary that runs offline, enabling local code completion and automation without network access. This design minimizes external dependencies and enhances privacy, though updates may be less frequent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ante 将编码代理打包成一个单独的二进制文件实现离线运行，支持本地代码补全与自动化。该设计降低对外部依赖、提升隐私，但更新可能不如在线方案频繁。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f213ec80ec9577d0e1d057c5af39e34b558dd132f999e1afb2f43a205292da09/AntigmaLabs/ante' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f213ec80ec9577d0e1d057c5af39e34b558dd132f999e1afb2f43a205292da09/AntigmaLabs/ante' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49173107' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://huggingface.co/LiquidAI/LFM2.5-2.6B' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LFM2.5 2.6B model competitive with 4x larger models</a></p>

<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://worm.net/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Worms: The Future of Yesterday&#x27;s Worms Today</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 07, 2026 / 04:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49208693' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49208693' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49208693'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece discusses how early ideas about worms—self-replicating software—still influence today&#x27;s malware and defensive tech, while innovations in automation reshape what &#x27;worm&#x27; means in software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨了“虫子（Worms）”这一概念在今日软件与网络安全中的延续与转变。早期蠕虫的思想如何影响当前的恶意软件与防御技术，以及自动化进化对“虫子”这一术语的新含义。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49324087' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://daringfireball.net/2026/08/anthropics_watermark_text_adulteration_in_claude_is_a_perversion_of_writing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic&#x27;s &#x27;Watermark&#x27; Text Adulteration in Claude Is a Perversion of Writing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 的 Claude 水印文本篡改是对写作的亵渎</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 14:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49324087' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49324087' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49324087'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece condemns Claude’s watermark-based text adulteration as undermining writing quality and integrity, arguing that watermarking AI-produced text distorts authorship and trust. It situates watermarking within broader debates about authenticity, control, and the consequences of AI-generated content in professional writing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章批评 Claude 的水印文本篡改扭曲写作质量与诚信，认为对 AI 产出文本打水印会损害署名与信任。讨论将水印纳入关于真实性、控制权及 AI 内容在专业写作中的影响的广泛议题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://daringfireball.net/graphics/df-wide-card.png' data-full='https://daringfireball.net/graphics/df-wide-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(201) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wcax.com/2026/08/07/kinney-drugs-pulls-back-ai-phone-assistant-after-hundreds-customer-complaints/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kinney Drugs pulls back AI phone assistant after hundreds of customer complaints</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kinney Drugs 在数百起客户投诉后撤回人工智能电话助手</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 07:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49244569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49244569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kinney Drugs paused its AI-powered phone assistant after hundreds of complaints, highlighting the challenges of deploying voice assistants in customer service. The episode underscores the need for robust testing, privacy safeguards, and clear failure modes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kinney Drugs 在遭遇大量投诉后，撤回了其 AI 电话助手。事件凸显了将语音助手用于客服场景的挑战，以及对质量控制和用户测试的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kibotronics.net/unlisted/lilly-machines/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>John C. Lilly on solid state intelligence and the elimination of man (1978)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>约翰·C·莉莉关于固态智能与消除人类的观点（1978）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 06:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49231397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 147</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49231397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49231397'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on John C. Lilly&#x27;s 1978 thoughts about &#x27;solid state intelligence&#x27; and the prospect of eliminating human labor or presence in intelligent systems. The material foreshadows later debates about machine autonomy and the societal consequences of AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾约翰·C·莉莉在1978年提出的“固态智能”设想，及其关于让人类在智能系统中被边缘化甚至替代的前瞻性讨论。相关观点预示了日后关于机器自治与人工智能对社会影响的辩论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49197565' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49197565' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://refp.se/articles/your-shell-and-the-lazy-exclamation-mark' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A shell exclamation mark is not for yelling. Be lazy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Shell 的感叹号并非用来喊话——要尽量简洁</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 06, 2026 / 07:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49197565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 144</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49197565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49197565'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that shell history expansion via the exclamation mark is a trap for surprising bugs and should be treated with caution. It recommends disabling history expansion or adopting safer defaults to avoid &#x27;!&#x27; pitfalls in daily workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章指出，shell 的历史扩展符号“!” 可能带来意外的命令执行错误，因此应谨慎对待。建议禁用历史扩展或采用更安全的默认设置，以避免日常工作中的坑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://refp.se/logo.png' data-full='https://refp.se/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49238050' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49238050' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(204) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.snowflake.com/en/blog/engineering/postgres-to-snowflake-replication-mirroring/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How We Pushed CDC into Postgres</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们如何把 CDC 引入 Postgres</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 18:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49238050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 144</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49238050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49238050'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post details enabling Change Data Capture (CDC) within Postgres to feed downstream systems, and how this pairs with PostgreSQL-to-Snowflake replication. It shows architectural decisions for near-real-time analytics across heterogeneous data stores.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该博文讲述在 Postgres 内实现变更数据捕获（CDC）的做法，分享了实现实时数据复制到 Snowflake 等下游系统的架构与工具选择，强调跨异构存储保持数据一致性的要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.snowflake.com/content/dam/snowflake-site/engineering-blog/featured-images/sf-eng-blog-ML-2.png' data-full='https://www.snowflake.com/content/dam/snowflake-site/engineering-blog/featured-images/sf-eng-blog-ML-2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49235011' data-tags='Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49235011' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/britain/2026/08/06/the-tragedy-of-the-commons-ai-edition' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The tragedy of the commons, AI edition</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI时代的公地悲剧</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 12:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49235011' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 144</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49235011' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49235011'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI introduces shared resources—compute, data, and access to models—that can be overused without governance. The column weighs policy and market options to prevent a classic tragedy of the commons in AI development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 时代的公地包括计算资源、数据和模型访问等共享要素，若缺乏治理容易被过度利用或垄断。文章探讨通过政策与市场手段避免AI发展中的公地悲剧。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A candid rant about oversized pull requests slowing progress, with tips on review throughput, smaller batches, and better collaboration practices to keep teams productive.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对超大 Pull Request 拖慢进度的直白抱怨，提出提升评审吞吐量、分批提交和更好协作的建议，以保持团队效率。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49277952' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49277952' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://brandur.org/fragments/postgres-without-pgbouncer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Does anyone run Postgres without PgBouncer?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>有人在不使用 PgBouncer 的情况下运行 Postgres 吗？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 13:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49277952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 142</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49277952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49277952'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Some teams run Postgres directly without PgBouncer, arguing that modern connection pools and well-tuned workloads can avoid the extra hop.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一些团队直接在 Postgres 上工作，不使用 PgBouncer，认为在经过优化的并发场景中可以避免额外的连接池开销。文中讨论连接开销、连接池生命周期，以及在高并发环境中 PgBouncer 是否仍具备价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://brandur.org/assets/images/favicon/favicon-256.jpg' data-full='https://brandur.org/assets/images/favicon/favicon-256.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49321353' data-tags='Science,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49321353' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/nih-ending-key-grant-budding-clinical-researchers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NIH is ending a key grant for budding clinical researchers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NIH 结束面向新兴临床研究者的关键资助</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 09:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49321353' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49321353' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49321353'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NIH is ending a key grant that supported aspiring clinical researchers. The move risks thinning the pipeline of early-career clinician-scientists and may force institutions to pivot training programs or seek alternative funding, potentially slowing translational research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NIH 将停止一项支持初级临床研究者的关键资助，可能削弱早期临床研究人员的培养通道。机构可能被迫重新分配经费或调整培训计划，若缺乏新的替代支持，或会拖慢转化研究的进展。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49285718' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49285718' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(209) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alexmorleyfinch.github.io/marlin/history/v1/article/the_birth.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I built a 500k-domain search engine for makers in a weekend for $10</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>周末用 10 美元建立一个面向 Maker 的 50 万域名搜索引擎</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 06:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49285718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 140</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49285718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49285718'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A weekend project yields a 500k-domain search engine for makers built for about $10. It demonstrates that low-cost, DIY search tooling can scale to half a million domains, highlighting the power of open data and simple tooling for niche communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个周末项目用约 10 美元打造了一个包含 50 万域名的 Maker 搜索引擎，展示了低成本自建工具的可行性。通过利用现成数据源和简单工具，实现了对小众社区有用的强大搜索能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://alexmorleyfinch.github.io/marlin/history/v1/article/images/hero-search.png' data-full='https://alexmorleyfinch.github.io/marlin/history/v1/article/images/hero-search.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49306443' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49306443' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(210) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.ploopy.co/the-aplus-is-finally-here-499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Ploopy A+ Trackball Is Here</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2018 年的研究提出对入门微积分的简化与重构，强调以直观基础为出发点，逐步引入抽象以降低学习难度。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49269429' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(212) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://funcall.blogspot.com/2026/08/why-vibe-code-in-lisp.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Target Common Lisp for Code Generation?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何将 Common Lisp 作为代码生成目标？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 01:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49269429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49269429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 118</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49269429'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Common Lisp&#x27;s powerful macro system, homoiconicity, and metaprogramming enable rapid experimentation and highly customizable code generators. Using Lisp as a target can simplify building abstractions, allowing developers to generate correct-by-construction code and iterate quickly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Common Lisp 的强大宏系统、同构表示和元编程能力，使其在代码生成方面具备快速试验和高可定制性的潜力。 将 Lisp 作为目标语言可以简化抽象层次的构建，使代码生成更加正确性可构造、迭代更高效。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49242698' data-tags='Culture,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49242698' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(213) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antiqua%E2%80%93Fraktur_dispute' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Antiqua–Fraktur dispute</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Antiqua–Fraktur 之争</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 05:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49242698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 137</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49242698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49242698'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Antiqua–Fraktur dispute was a long-running debate over whether German texts should use Antiqua (Roman type) or Fraktur (blackletter). It shaped publishing, education, and national identity, culminating in shifts in official typography in the 20th century.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Antiqua–Fraktur 之争是关于德国文本应采用 Antiqua（罗马体）还是 Fraktur（黑体字）的长期文化辩论。它影响了印刷、教育与国家身份认同，直到20世纪的官方字体政策才发生转变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c0/Schriftzug_Antiqua.svg/1280px-Schriftzug_Antiqua.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=index&amp;utm_content=thumbnail' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c0/Schriftzug_Antiqua.svg/1280px-Schriftzug_Antiqua.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=index&amp;utm_content=thumbnail' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(224) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.redblobgames.com/blog/2026-08-08-differential-heuristics/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Differential Heuristics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>差分启发式算法</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 06:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49231490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49231490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49231490'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into using differential heuristics to guide search or optimization, allowing more accurate or faster estimates by comparing neighboring states. It shows practical examples in pathfinding or AI planning, and how small perturbations yield better pruning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍如何通过对相邻状态估值的对比使用差分启发式来提升搜索和规划效率，能更快地做出更准确的剪枝判断。结合路径查找等场景给出实际应用与实现要点。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thephd.dev/to-save-c-we-must-save-abi-fixing-c-function-abi' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>To save C, we must save ABI (2022)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为拯救 C，我们必须拯救 ABI（2022）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 08, 2026 / 00:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49219679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49219679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49219679'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay argues that saving the C language requires reviving its ABI, the binary interface that governs function calls and data layout, to prevent fragmentation and enable portable, reliable software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张要拯救 C 语言，必须重建其 ABI，即控制函数调用和数据布局的二进制接口，以避免碎片化并实现可移植、可靠的软件。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://thephd.dev//assets/img/2022/03/lilwilbug%20-%20eyes%20open.jpg' data-full='https://thephd.dev//assets/img/2022/03/lilwilbug%20-%20eyes%20open.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49322573' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49322573' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://buf.build/blog/protobuf-lsp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Protobuf has LSP support. You&#x27;re welcome</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Protobuf 已支持 LSP（语言服务器协议）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 11:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49322573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49322573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49322573'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Protobuf now has built-in LSP support, enabling editors to provide autocompletion, diagnostics, and refactoring for protobuf schemas. That reduces friction for developers who model APIs and data contracts, potentially speeding up schema evolution and integration across services. This aligns protobuf with other IDLs bridging IDE tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Protobuf 现在原生支持 LSP（语言服务器协议），让编辑器能够对 protobuf 架构提供自动补全、诊断和重构等功能，减少 API 与数据契约建模时的摩擦，助力跨服务的模式演进与集成。这也使 protobuf 在 IDE 工具链方面与其他 IDL 看齐。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://buf.build/images/cms/blog/69c3092bd98bec6b03e4d7e5_blog-protobuf-finally-has.png' data-full='https://buf.build/images/cms/blog/69c3092bd98bec6b03e4d7e5_blog-protobuf-finally-has.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49290768' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49290768' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cdn.openai.com/pdf/how-organizations-use-chatgpt.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Organizations Use AI: Evidence from ChatGPT [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>组织如何使用AI：基于ChatGPT的证据（PDF）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 12:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49290768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49290768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49290768'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The PDF collects empirical evidence on how organizations deploy ChatGPT, revealing common use cases like customer support and knowledge work. It documents adoption patterns, governance practices, and the tradeoffs between productivity gains and risks such as data leakage and misalignment. The takeaway is that successful AI adoption hinges on structured evaluation, policy controls, and cross-functional collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该 PDF 汇总了组织部署 ChatGPT 的实证证据，揭示了客户支持与知识工作等常见用例。它记录了采用模式、治理做法，以及生产力提升与数据泄露、目标错位等风险之间的权衡。关键在于成功落地依赖于结构化评估、政策控制和跨职能协作。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(237) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/how-godels-proof-works-20200714/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Gödel&#x27;s Proof Works (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>哥德尔证明如何起作用（2020）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 12:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49290969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 115</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49290969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49290969'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article unpacks Gödel&#x27;s incompleteness theorem in accessible terms, showing how self-reference and arithmetization create a fundamental limit on provability. It also places the result in the context of mathematics, logic, and AI, illustrating why these limits matter for formal systems and algorithms. Understanding this helps frame debates about consistency, completeness, and the limits of automated reasoning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章用通俗方式梳理哥德尔不完备定理，展示自指和算术化如何在形式系统中造成可证明性的根本极限。并将这一结果放在数学、逻辑与人工智能的语境中，解释这些限制为何影响算法与形式系统设计。理解这一点有助于认识一致性、完备性以及自动推理的边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d2r55xnwy6nx47.cloudfront.net/uploads/2020/07/Godel-Tower_1200_social.jpg' data-full='https://d2r55xnwy6nx47.cloudfront.net/uploads/2020/07/Godel-Tower_1200_social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49226563'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Medical consensus on knuckles suggests cracking won&#x27;t cause long-term damage in most cases, but may cause short-term swelling or grip issues. If anxious, consider alternatives like stress relief techniques; keep sessions moderate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于捏指节的医学共识：长期损伤的证据不充分，但频繁捏指可能导致肿胀、疼痛或握力下降。若担心影响，建议用替代放松方式和适度的练习。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 致德州州长阿博特的信：推动负责任的 AI 基础设施</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 07:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49244308' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49244308' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 217</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49244308'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI sent a letter to Texas Governor Abbott urging a framework for responsible AI infrastructure, balancing innovation with safety and accountability. It calls for data governance, transparent deployment, and local AI adoption with guardrails.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 给德州州长阿博特发函，呼吁在 AI 基础设施建设中遵循负责任原则，兼顾创新、安全与问责。信中提出数据治理、透明度以及在地方层面推动 AI 部署的框架与 guardrails。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(248) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phrack.org/issues/72/19#article' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Hacker&#x27;s Renaissance (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑客的文艺复兴</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49235378' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 111</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49235378' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49235378'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>There&#x27;s a renewed energy in hacker culture, fueled by open tooling, community labs, and responsible disclosure. It&#x27;s shaping security research into a collaborative, ethics-focused movement rather than a lone-wolf craft.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客社区正呈现复兴态势，得益于开源工具、社区实验室和负责任披露文化。安全研究变得更具协作性和伦理性，不再只是个体的技术探索。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49315995'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It touches on visual perception tricks, potential low-cost guidance strategies, and the limits of such approaches in real-world conditions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章探讨了一个有趣的想法：用莫尔纹作为简单的可视指引来引导船舶，展示如何通过图案设计影响导航信号，而非依赖复杂设备。讨论了视觉感知的巧思、潜在的低成本引导策略，以及在现实环境中的局限性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 06:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49243687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49243687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49243687'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A math blog post explores the rich lattice structure of sets of natural numbers, shedding light on the depth of order-theoretic properties in a seemingly simple domain. It highlights how foundational combinatorics can reveal surprising complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数学博客探讨自然数集合的格结构的丰富性，揭示一个看似简单的领域也隐藏着深刻的序理论性质。文章强调基础组合学中可揭示的意想不到的复杂性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Horseshoe crab blue blood contains Limulus amebocyte lysate used in endotoxin testing, enabling safe vaccines and medical equipment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>马蹄蟹蓝血中含有用于内毒素检测的 Limulus amebocyte lysate，支撑疫苗和医疗器械的安全性。随着采血对物种的威胁日益显现，研究者正加紧研发合成替代品，以兼顾医学需要与物种保护。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen 3.8 27B：出色但默认易过度推理</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 16:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49324985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49324985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49324985'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen 3.8 27B performs strongly, but its default prompting overemphasizes exhaustive reasoning, slowing or muddying responses. The author discusses practical tweaks to get clearer, faster outputs without sacrificing accuracy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen 3.8 27B 性能出色，但默认设置容易走向冗长推理，影响输出的清晰度与速度。文中给出在不牺牲准确性的前提下提高回答简洁性的实用调整。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49319349' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49319349' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(285) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ei3lh.eu/2026/08/16/a-true-telnet-bbs-on-a-casio-calculator/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A True Telnet BBS on a Casio Calculator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Casio 计算器上实现的真实 Telnet BBS</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 05:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49319349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49319349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49319349'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Casio calculator runs a true Telnet BBS, a surprising feat of tiny-device networking and retro-computing prowess. The project highlights how small hardware can push real-world networking and hobbyist BBS culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在一台 Casio 计算器上竟然运行着真实的 Telnet BBS，显示了极小设备网络能力的边界和复古计算的魅力。该项目凸显了在资源受限设备上实现实际网络服务的可能性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ei3lh.eu/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/1000013219-1024x683.png' data-full='https://ei3lh.eu/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/1000013219-1024x683.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 20:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49281352' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49281352' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49281352'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Clamiga brings Common Lisp to the Amiga platform, offering a modern Lisp environment on this classic hardware. The project bridges retro computing enthusiasm with contemporary language tooling, showing that old machines can still run productive software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Clamiga 让 Common Lisp 重返 Amiga 平台，提供在这台经典硬件上运行的现代 Lisp 环境。这个项目把复古计算热情与当代语言工具结合起来，显示了老机器仍然可以生产出有用的软件。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 17:36 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49237873'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Japanese court overturned the Red RAW video patent, potentially clearing obstacles for camera makers to implement RAW video without licensing barriers. The decision has implications for open formats and licensing costs in the imaging stack.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本法院推翻了 Red RAW 视频专利，可能消除相机厂商在实现 RAW 视频时的许可风险，降低合规成本。事件对开放格式的使用和未来专利授权生态具有重要影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 07:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49272620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49272620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49272620'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A German advocacy group files criminal complaint over Meta&#x27;s AI glasses, raising privacy and surveillance concerns. The action signals regulatory scrutiny of wearable AI devices and potential legal risks for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德国一个倡导团体就 Meta 的 AI 眼镜提起刑事起诉，聚焦隐私与监控问题。此举表明对可穿戴 AI 设备的监管关注正在升温，且对开发者构成潜在法律风险。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49230630' data-tags='Finance,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49230630' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(291) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=NufJ7g63KSY' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Wall Street is ignoring big tech&#x27;s debt [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>华尔街为何忽视科技巨头的债务</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 05:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49230630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49230630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49230630'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains why investors and credit markets have largely ignored heavyweight debt from Big Tech, despite rising interest costs. The talk argues that high growth, strong cash flows, and AI-driven monetization keep debt manageable, while market structure and risk appetite drive resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阐明了为何华尔街对科技巨头的债务反应冷却——尽管融资成本上升，投资者仍相对乐观。文中指出高增长、强劲现金流以及 AI 驱动的盈利前景使得债务负担可控，同时市场结构和风险偏好也在支撑其耐受性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49232870' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(292) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/carpdiem/ember' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Ember – Redshift safe color palettes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ember——适用于 Redshift 的安全调色板</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 09:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49232870'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ember is a collection of color palettes designed to be safe for Redshift rendering, emphasizing colorblind accessibility and perceptual uniformity. It helps data viz creators avoid misleading palettes and speeds up choosing ready-to-use schemes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ember 提供一组适合 Redshift 渲染的调色板，强调色盲友好与感知一致性，帮助数据可视化设计者避免误导性配色并快速选用现成配色。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49268565' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49268565' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(293) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08421' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A SAT Attack on Tarski&#x27;s High School Algebra Problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对塔尔斯基高中代数题的 SAT 攻击</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 23:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49268565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49268565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49268565'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A SAT-based attack demonstrates how modern SAT solvers can tackle a classic algebra problem from Tarski, revealing insights about encoding strategies and solver limitations. The work shows the boundary between human-style reasoning and automated SAT reasoning for math problems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SAT 求解器在解决塔尔斯基的高中代数题上展现出新的能力，揭示了编码策略和求解器的局限性。该研究凸显自动推理在数学题上的边界，以及人类直觉与机器推理之间的差异。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49319727' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(294) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rand.org/pubs/research_reports/RRA4335-1.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A U.S. Strategy to Prevent the Creation of Mirror Life</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国防止镜像生命产生的策略</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 06:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49319727' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49319727' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49319727'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RAND outlines strategies to preempt the creation of self-replicating &#x27;mirror&#x27; life forms, covering regulatory, biosafety, and governance dimensions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RAND 提出预防‘镜像生命’产生的策略，涵盖法规、生物安全与治理等方面，讨论在前沿合成生物研究中的风险缓释。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(295) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aimagazine.com/news/why-did-openai-head-of-ethics-chloe-bakalar-leave' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Did OpenAI&#x27;s Head of Ethics Chloé Bakalar Leave?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 伦理主管 Chloé Bakalar 离职原因</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 07:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49258581' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49258581' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49258581'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI’s head of ethics Chloé Bakalar has departed, stirring questions about how the company manages safety and governance as AI systems scale. While official reasons are sparse, industry chatter hints at tensions over safety programs and strategic direction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 伦理主管 Chloé Bakalar 离职，引发关于公司在 AI 安全与治理方面的处理方式的讨论。官方信息有限，业内普遍将其与安全计划及战略方向的分歧联系起来。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49183917' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49183917' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(296) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=fow-LsdaH2E' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Blackwing Pencils are Made [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Blackwing 铅笔的制作过程</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 05, 2026 / 07:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49183917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49183917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49183917'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This behind-the-scenes video reveals Blackwing pencils&#x27; production, from wood sourcing to graphite cores and ferrule design. It highlights craftsmanship and manufacturing choices that give premium pencils their distinctive character.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这段幕后视频揭示 Blackwing 铅笔的生产流程，从木材来源、石墨芯到金属夹环的工艺细节。通过展现制造环节，显示出高端铅笔的匠心与供应链选择。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(300) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Tragedy of the Cognitive Commons</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>认知公共资源的悲剧</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 08:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49245359' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49245359' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49245359'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A theoretical piece argues that shared cognitive resources—data, compute, attention—can suffer from misaligned incentives, risking collective intelligence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇理论文章指出，数据、算力与注意力等共享认知资源若激励错位，可能带来集体智能的风险。文中讨论了对 AI 治理与跨机构协作的影响。</p>
