</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49314596' data-tags='Science,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49314596' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(301) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wow!_signal' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Wow signal was a strong narrowband radio signal detected on August 15, 1977</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wow信号：1977年8月15日探测到的强烈窄带无线电信号</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 15, 2026 / 14:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49314596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49314596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49314596'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Wow signal remains one of the most compelling candidate extraterrestrial signals, a strong narrowband radio emission detected in 1977. Revisitings of its significance highlight how rare, longshot signals can fuel scientific curiosity and methods for distinguishing noise from potential alien tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wow信号是至今最具争议性的外星信号候选之一，1977年观测到的强烈窄带射电发射。对其意义的再评估凸显罕见信号对科学探索的驱动作用，以及如何區分噪声与潜在的外星信号的方法论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/d/d3/Wow_signal.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=index&amp;utm_content=thumbnail_unscaled' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/d/d3/Wow_signal.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=index&amp;utm_content=thumbnail_unscaled' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49310829' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49310829' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(302) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://printytron.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Printytron – Describe a part, get a printable STL</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Printytron——描述一个部件，得到可打印的 STL 文件</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 15, 2026 / 07:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49310829' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49310829' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49310829'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Printytron converts a textual part description into an STL file for 3D printing, enabling rapid prototyping without CAD expertise. It demonstrates how natural-language interfaces can streamline hardware iteration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Printytron 可以把部件描述转化为可用于3D打印的 STL 文件，降低了原型设计的门槛。展示了自然语言界面在硬件迭代中的应用潜力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49280047'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Adopt a platform-wide, modular approach that prioritizes broad capability before shipping narrow, coherent releases. The strategy reduces risk by decoupling components and accelerates customer value through focused, early releases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>广泛构建的平台化、模块化方法，先追求广泛能力再发布小而完整的版本。通过解耦组件来降低风险，并通过聚焦的早期版本提升用户价值。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 04:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49242245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49242245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49242245'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Because It&#x27;s Not Fun Enough: why languages fail argues that developer happiness matters as much as features, performance, and ecosystems. It examines the trade-offs that lead languages to stagnate when the experience isn&#x27;t enjoyable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章主张开发者体验是语言成败的关键因素之一，指出若缺乏乐趣和易用性，语言生态容易停滞。它分析了 ergonomics、性能与生态系统之间的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49182148' data-tags='Programming,Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49182148' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(307) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gitlab.com/Kazade/picophysics' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Picophysics: Single file physics for games on platforms like N64, PSX, DC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Picophysics：适用于 N64、PSX、Dreamcast 等平台的单文件物理引擎</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 05, 2026 / 05:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49182148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49182148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49182148'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Picophysics is a compact, single-file physics engine designed for constrained hardware (N64, PSX, Dreamcast). It emphasizes portability and determinism, helping indie developers add physics to retro-style games with minimal dependencies. The project demonstrates that a tiny footprint can still deliver believable simulations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Picophysics 是面向资源受限硬件（如 N64、PSX、Dreamcast）的单文件物理引擎。它强调可移植性和确定性，帮助独立开发者在复古风格游戏中加入物理效果，且无需额外依赖。该项目展示了极简实现也能实现可信的物理仿真。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gitlab.com/assets/twitter_card-570ddb06edf56a2312253c5872489847a0f385112ddbcd71ccfa1570febab5d2.jpg' data-full='https://gitlab.com/assets/twitter_card-570ddb06edf56a2312253c5872489847a0f385112ddbcd71ccfa1570febab5d2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49258202' data-tags='Culture,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49285096' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49285096' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(312) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pikuma.com/blog/programming-intel-pentium-mmx-simd' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SIMD in the 90s: Programming Intel&#x27;s Pentium MMX</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>90年代的 SIMD：在英特尔 Pentium MMX 上的编程</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 05:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49285096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49285096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49285096'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on 1990s SIMD shows how Intel&#x27;s Pentium MMX introduced integer SIMD on x86, but with design constraints that limited portability and performance. The piece explains how MMX influenced later generations (SSE/AVX) and highlights early compiler and ISA trade-offs that still matter for modern vectorization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 90 年代的 SIMD，介绍英特尔 Pentium MMX 如何在 x86 上引入整型向量指令，以及设计约束带来的可移植性与性能局限。文中阐述 MMX 如何影响了后续的 SSE/AVX，以及早期编译器与 ISA 的权衡，这些对如今的矢量化仍具启示作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pikuma.com/images/blog/programming-intel-pentium-mmx-simd/thumbnail-1200x630.jpg' data-full='https://pikuma.com/images/blog/programming-intel-pentium-mmx-simd/thumbnail-1200x630.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tess 的 Android Wayland 合成器项目致力于在 Android 上实现 Wayland 合成器，可能带来原生 Wayland 界面和更平滑的图形栈。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(320) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ntfy.sh/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ntfy – open-source Push to Mobile</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ntfy：开源推送至移动端</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 03:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49296902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49296902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49296902'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ntfy is an open-source push notification server and client ecosystem, enabling self-hosted, private notifications to mobile devices with lightweight clients and simple API. It’s attractive for teams seeking privacy-respecting, offline-first messaging without vendor lock-in.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ntfy 是一个开源推送通知服务器及客户端生态，支持自托管、私有通知推送到移动端，API 简单且轻量。对追求隐私、离线优先且不依赖厂商锁定的团队尤为有吸引力。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49295238' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(321) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.elttam.com/blog/ruby-4-0-universal-rce-deserialization-gadget-chain' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ruby 4.0 Universal RCE Deserialization Gadget Chain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ruby 4.0 通用远程代码执行反序列化 gadget 链</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 23:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49295238' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49295238' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49295238'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical security write-up reveals a universal gadget chain in Ruby 4.0 enabling remote code execution via deserialization. It underscores the dangers of deserializing untrusted data and prompts developers to avoid risky constructs or harden against gadget chains in libraries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇关键安全分析揭示 Ruby 4.0 中存在的通用 gadget 链，可能通过反序列化实现远程代码执行。这凸显了不信任数据反序列化的风险，敦促开发者避免易受 gadget 链影响的用法并对库进行加固。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6971f0e051b588235e8acf7b/6a7e91f27de7b76cbfa2ea50_image%20(1).png' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6971f0e051b588235e8acf7b/6a7e91f27de7b76cbfa2ea50_image%20(1).png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>购买 5,000 本冷僻书名的神秘大单引发怀疑</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49242213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49242213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A mysterious bulk order of 5,000 obscure book titles has sparked suspicion about provenance or data-related schemes. It underscores how seemingly mundane metadata can trigger questions about supply chains and licensing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一笔神秘的大宗订单购买了 5,000 本冷僻书名，引发对来源与授权的怀疑。事件凸显了看似普通的元数据背后可能的隐匿供应链问题。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(325) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/digital-signal-processing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bede Liu, a digital signal processing pioneer, has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数字信号处理领域的先驱比德·刘去世</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 15, 2026 / 14:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49314615' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49314615' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49314615'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bede Liu, a pioneer in digital signal processing, has died. His foundational contributions shaped modern DSP and influenced generations of researchers in communications, audio processing, and related fields. The obit honors a career that bridged theory and practical engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数字信号处理领域的先驱比德·刘已去世。他在DSP的基础性贡献深刻影响了现代通信、音频处理及相关领域，培养了无数研究者。此篇讣告致敬一位将理论与工程实践相结合的杰出学者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/portrait-of-an-elderly-asian-man-softly-smiling-in-a-suit-jacket-and-tie.jpg?id=67609508&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C125%2C0%2C125' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/portrait-of-an-elderly-asian-man-softly-smiling-in-a-suit-jacket-and-tie.jpg?id=67609508&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C125%2C0%2C125' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49306333' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(326) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://studios.disneyresearch.com/2026/07/16/2d-gaussian-splatting-for-bezier-spline-line-art-vectorization/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>2D Gaussian Splatting for Bézier Spline Line Art Vectorization</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用于贝塞尔样条线艺术矢量化的二维高斯喷涂</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 17:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49306333' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49306333' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49306333'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Disney Research introduces a 2D Gaussian splatting approach to vectorize raster Bézier spline line art, producing smooth, scalable vectors from pixel-based input. The technique could streamline auto-vectorization in design and animation workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>迪士尼研究提出一种二维高斯喷涂方法，将栅格化的贝塞尔样条线艺术矢量化，生成来自像素输入的平滑、可扩展向量。该技术有望简化设计与动画工作流中的自动矢量化过程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://studios.disneyresearch.com/app/uploads/2025/03/DRS-Mickey_logo-full-color_black-2.png' data-full='https://studios.disneyresearch.com/app/uploads/2025/03/DRS-Mickey_logo-full-color_black-2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(327) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://commonreader.wustl.edu/winning-and-losing-at-the-great-game-of-intimacy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anton Chekhov played at love most of his life</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>契诃夫一生把爱情演得最为重要</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 16:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49306021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49306021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49306021'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chekhov&#x27;s enduring preoccupation with love shaped his writing, with letters and anecdotes revealing how longing and restraint mingled with humor and human insight. The piece suggests intimate life experiences fed the tenderness and irony that define his work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为契诃夫一生把爱情作为创作与生活的重要主题，书信与轶事揭示了情感渴望、克制与幽默如何融入他的笔触。爱情经历影响了他作品中的语气、洞察与对人性的描摹。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49211158' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49211158' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(328) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/halcyon-video/halcyon-video' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Halcyon Video – a 3D video store for your media server</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Halcyon Video：面向本地媒体服务器的 3D 视频商店</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 07, 2026 / 07:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49211158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49211158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49211158'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Halcyon Video is a 3D video store for your media server, offering a curated catalog or streaming of 3D content. This project highlights how home media servers can adopt more immersive formats and manage rights, transcoding, and metadata for 3D content.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Halcyon Video 是一个面向本地媒体服务器的 3D 视频商店，旨在提供对3D 内容的管理与流式传输。该项目展示了家庭媒体服务器在沉浸式格式上的应用潜力，并涉及对 3D 内容的编目、转码与元数据管理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6fe275728bb26335b18ca35ccdd0bec61f160624f70e56127eca15f66fc319a7/halcyon-video/halcyon-video' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6fe275728bb26335b18ca35ccdd0bec61f160624f70e56127eca15f66fc319a7/halcyon-video/halcyon-video' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49191130' data-tags='Programming,Design,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49191130' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(329) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hazyresearch.stanford.edu/blog/2026-08-05-retire-the-abstractions' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Retire the Abstractions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>淘汰抽象化</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 05, 2026 / 18:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49191130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49191130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49191130'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Retire the Abstractions argues for simplifying software layers and rethinking abstraction in AI/system design to reduce fragility and improve reliability. It advocates more explicit data-centric or component-level reasoning and cautions against over-abstracted architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章主张简化软件层次、重新评估 AI/系统设计中的抽象程度，以降低脆弱性并提升可靠性。作者倡导以数据驱动或组件级推理为核心，警惕过度抽象带来的隐患。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hazyresearch.stanford.edu/hazy-logo.png' data-full='https://hazyresearch.stanford.edu/hazy-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPS 与失去的迷路艺术</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 07:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49320673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49320673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49320673'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article argues that GPS has eroded our natural sense of direction, diminishing navigational skills and cognitive mapping. It traces the evolution of cartography and weighs the trade-offs between convenience and the value of exploratory uncertainty.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为依赖 GPS 降低了人们在没有数字导航时的定向能力，并追溯了地图学和认知地图的历史。便捷性与探索精神的价值之间，需要权衡对导航技能与个人空间的影响。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(339) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://therad.ninja/from-8-10-seconds-to-3-7-teaching-automatic1111-to-speak-metal-on-an-m3-pro/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Automatic1111 for Apple metal, 40% speed up sd1.5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Automatic1111 在 Apple Metal 上的降速加速，sd1.5 提升约 40%</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 06:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49272282' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49272282' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49272282'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An optimization shows Automatic1111 running on Apple Metal yields roughly 40% faster inference for Stable Diffusion 1.5 on Apple Silicon. The note demonstrates how hardware-accelerated rendering can meaningfully speed up AI image generation without changing models. It may influence developers to adopt Metal optimization in local workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Apple Metal 上运行的 Automatic1111 对 Stable Diffusion 1.5 提升了约 40% 的推理速度，适用于 Apple Silicon 设备。该优化展示了硬件加速在本地 AI 图像生成中的实用性，可能促使开发者在本地工作流中采用 Metal 优化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49269451' data-tags='Programming,Web,Science,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49269451' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(340) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://notebook.link/blog/numba-in-the-browser/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Numba in the Browser: Unlocking a New Scientific Python Stack in JupyterLite</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>浏览器中的 Numba：在 JupyterLite 中解锁全新的科学 Python 堆栈</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 01:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49269451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49269451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49269451'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Numba, the JIT compiler for Python, runs in the browser via WebAssembly in JupyterLite, enabling accelerated numeric code in a portable Python environment. This brings performance-friendly scientific Python workflows to browser notebooks, lowering barriers for experimentation offline and in education. It also highlights integration challenges around memory, typing, and compatibility with numpy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在浏览器中通过 WebAssembly 让 Numba 能在 JupyterLite 里运行，提供更高性能的科学 Python 代码在便携环境中的执行。让浏览器笔记本具备更接近本地的数值计算能力，适合离线实验和教育场景，同时也要面临内存、类型系统和对 NumPy 的兼容性等挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://notebook.link/blog/img/NotebookLink/' data-full='https://notebook.link/blog/img/NotebookLink/' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(341) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-10857-1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Superconducting monolayer cuprate with a single CuO2 plane</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>仅含一个 CuO2 平面的超导单层铜氧化物</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 12:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49277153' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49277153' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49277153'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers report a superconducting monolayer cuprate with a single CuO2 plane, pushing the boundaries of two-dimensional superconductivity. The finding deepens understanding of high-temperature superconductors and could influence how we design ultra-thin superconducting devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究报告仅含一个 CuO2 平面的超导单层铜氧化物，推动对二维超导现象的理解。此发现可能影响高温超导理论及超薄超导器件的设计。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49241559' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49241559' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(351) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cvgx4yd1gl2o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tech leaders say AI means less work – staff say they work up to 90 hours a week</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tech 领袖称 AI 能减少工作量 — 员工却每周工作的时长高达 90 小时</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 02:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49241559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49241559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49241559'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tech leaders claim AI reduces work, but frontline staff report pushing up to 90 hours per week, highlighting a gap between leadership expectations and day-to-day realities. The piece calls for realistic metrics and governance when deploying AI at scale. Takeaway: AI can shift workloads in ways that increase stress unless managed properly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科技领袖声称 AI 能减少工作量，但一线员工却报告每周工作时长高达90小时，揭示领导层预期与实际情况之间的错位。文章呼吁在大规模部署 AI 时采用现实的度量标准和治理。要点是：若管理不善，AI 可能把工作负担转嫁给员工，增加压力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/e1c0/live/bb9691e0-94b3-11f1-a7ab-8b30adf0800a.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/e1c0/live/bb9691e0-94b3-11f1-a7ab-8b30adf0800a.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这段复古科技视频讲述了诺维尔 NE2000 以太网网卡的传奇。凭借简易的设计和开源硬件，成为早期 PC 网络的标准之一，并影响了后续的兼容性与驱动模型。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49264583' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49264583' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(360) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Emergent Introspective Awareness in Large Language Models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型语言模型中的涌现式内省意识</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 14:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49264583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49264583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49264583'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A claim that large language models exhibit emergent introspective awareness—reflecting on their own reasoning and errors—as models scale, raising questions about interpretability and alignment. The work cautions that such abilities may be brittle and context-dependent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究提出大型语言模型在规模扩展时出现的涌现式内省意识，即对自身推理与错误进行反思的能力，引发对可解释性与对齐问题的讨论，同时也指出这类能力可能不稳定且依赖上下文。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49286424'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A graduate student proves a fractal uncertainty principle, linking fractal geometry with quantum harmonic analysis. The result tightens fundamental limits on simultaneous localization in a novel setting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位研究生证明了分形不确定性原理，将分形几何与量子调和分析联系起来。这一结果在新的情景中进一步限定了同时定位的基本极限。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 15, 2026 / 10:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49312496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49312496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49312496'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>South Korea proposed formal talks with North Korea to officially end the Korean War, a step toward lasting peace and reduced military tension on the peninsula. While symbolic, the move could realign regional security dynamics and impact cross-border cooperation and technology exchanges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>韩国提出与朝鲜就正式结束战争进行谈判，寻求实现持久和平并降低半岛紧张局势。此举虽具象征意义，但可能重塑区域安全格局并影响跨境合作与科技交流。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: MCP Memory—使用 OKF 与 SQLite FTS5 的高速代理内存</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 06:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49286073' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49286073' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MCP Memory introduces a fast agent memory system built with Google&#x27;s OKF and SQLite FTS5. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MCP Memory 提供一个基于 Google 的 OKF 和 SQLite FTS5 的高速代理记忆系统，定位于为 AI 代理提供低延迟的上下文记忆。该方案成本友好，可能影响开发者实现长期对话助手的方式。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(376) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bernsteinbear.com/blog/zkp/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A quick look at zero-knowledge proofs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>零知识证明速览</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 12:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49303776' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49303776' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49303776'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise primer on zero-knowledge proofs explains the core idea: prove knowledge of a secret without revealing it. It sketches how ZKPs work at a high level and highlights common applications in authentication and privacy-preserving systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>简明的零知识证明入门介绍核心思想：在不暴露秘密的前提下验证对秘密的掌握。概述了工作原理的要点，并指出在认证与隐私保护系统中的典型应用。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(377) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bengodfrey.dev/blog/process/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Process as a Proxy for Motivation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把过程当作激励的代理</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 15:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49279197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49279197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49279197'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shifting focus from outcomes to repeatable processes can sustain motivation and reduce cognitive load. The piece outlines practical tactics for turning goals into daily routines, providing feedback loops that keep teams moving even when priorities shift.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>把关注点从结果转向可重复执行的流程，可以提升动机并降低认知负担。 给出将目标转化为日常例行工作、形成反馈循环的实用方法，帮助团队在优先级变化时依然保持前进。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>偶像麻将最终恋情：伪装成电子游戏的幻灯片</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49236811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49236811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49236811'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that Idol Mahjong Final Romance is essentially a slideshow masquerading as a video game, using Mahjong idol aesthetics to entice rather than provide meaningful interactivity. The article riffs on the mismatch between glossy packaging and limited gameplay.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出《偶像麻将最终恋情》本质上是一部伪装成电子游戏的幻灯片展示，以偶像麻将美学吸引玩家而非提供真正的互动。文章还嘲讽其华丽包装与有限游戏性的错配。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(379) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ben-z/findphone' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Findphone: Locate a nearby Bluetooth device by signal strength</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 05, 2026 / 04:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49181584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49181584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49181584'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Findphone demonstrates locating nearby Bluetooth devices by signal strength, enabling approximate proximity without GPS. It discusses challenges like RSSI variability and the privacy implications of device discovery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Findphone 展示如何利用信号强度近似定位附近的蓝牙设备，而无需 GPS。文中讨论 RSSI 的波动性以及隐私与安全方面的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Digi-Comp 1 is a 1963 plastic mechanical computer, demonstrated in a video that shows how marbles and tubes implement logic. It&#x27;s a landmark in hands-on hardware education and a reminder of playful, tangible computing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Digi-Comp 1 是一台1963年的塑料机械计算机，视频演示通过小球和管道实现逻辑运算。它是动手硬件教育的里程碑，也提醒人们边玩边学的早期可触及计算。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A method to attribute edits between human and AI contributions at the line level using diffs, addressing accountability when agents edit text. The approach helps teams audit changes and understand authorship in collaborative writing.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A historical ethnographic text cataloging snake-based omens; the piece examines how 19th-century scholars compiled and interpreted non-Western knowledge systems, revealing biases and curiosity of the era. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这本19世纪的学者记录蛇卜的著作，描述了以蛇取兆的信仰体系。文章将它放在科学史和跨文化知识交流的语境中，揭示当时的世界观与偏见，以及对现代文本研究的启示。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(384) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/by-invitation/2026/08/13/the-case-for-overhauling-american-science' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The case for overhauling American science</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全面改革美国科学的必要性</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 16:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49305708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49305708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49305708'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Economist argues for a structural overhaul of American science policy, including funding, governance, and incentives to accelerate innovation. The piece outlines reforms to better align research with societal needs and global competitiveness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《经济学人》呼吁改革美国科学政策，涵盖资助、治理与激励机制，以推动创新更高效地落地。文中提出系统性改革以提升研究方向对社会需求和全球竞争力的贡献。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49303540' data-tags='AI,Security'>

<p class='hn-title'>(385) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/f61d49fa5596956a5dec75fea0e973bf6a6a8378/Redacted%20Risk%20Report%20August%202026%20.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic Risk August 2026 [pdf]</a></p>

<p class='hn-title'>(386) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://korben.info/en/france-tax-authority-data-breach-680000-taxpayers.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>France&#x27;s tax authority had data stolen on 680k taxpayers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国税务局数据被窃：68万名纳税人信息被盗</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 06:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49298303' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49298303' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49298303'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>France&#x27;s tax authority suffered a data breach exposing sensitive taxpayer information for about 680,000 individuals, highlighting persistent government cybersecurity risk and the human impact of breaches. The incident underscores the need for robust access controls, monitoring, and rapid breach notification.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国税务局披露约 68 万名纳税人敏感信息被窃，凸显政府级别的网络安全风险及对个人的潜在影响。事件强调加强访问控制、监控和快速披露的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>格陵兰对美国石油公司非法钻探提出强烈警告</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 07:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49258687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49258687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>格陵兰就一美国石油公司非法钻探发表强烈警告，突显北极资源开发中的主权与环境问题，以及对监管执行的关注。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49245348' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49245348' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(388) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.neurology.org/doi/10.1212/WN9.0000000000000152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Midlife Vascular Risk Burden and Dementia-Free Survival Years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>中年血管风险负担与无痴呆生存年限</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 08:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49245348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49245348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49245348'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A neurology study links higher midlife vascular risk burden to fewer dementia-free years, highlighting the long-term cognitive health impact of vascular health. The findings suggest preventive strategies could extend healthy, dementia-free life.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>中年期更高的血管风险负担与较短的无痴呆生存岁月相关，强调血管健康对长期认知健康的重要性。研究表明通过预防性干预有望延长健康、无痴呆的生活年限。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49193666' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49193666' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(389) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kieranvelasquez.substack.com/p/on-the-german-mittelstand' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The German Mittelstand</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德国 Mittelstand：中小企业的创新动能与经济支柱</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 06, 2026 / 00:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49193666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49193666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49193666'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The German Mittelstand—the backbone of Germany’s economy—continues to drive manufacturing excellence and exports, even as AI and demographic pressures mount.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德国的中小企业（Mittelstand）仍是经济与创新的主力，推动制造业出口与全球竞争力。文章分析了这些家族式企业如何在 AI、人口结构等挑战下维持生产力与长期治理。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(390) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/08/14/apple-proposes-to-take-a-15-cut-of-purchases-made-outside-the-app-store/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple proposes to take a 15% cut of purchases made outside the App Store</a></p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49204232' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(391) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tokyodev.com/articles/yes-and-what-an-improv-stage-can-teach-you-about-leading-cross-cultural-teams-in-tokyo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What an improv stage can teach you about leading cross-cultural teams in Tokyo</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>即兴舞台能教你在东京领导跨文化团队的道理</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 06, 2026 / 16:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49204232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49204232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49204232'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An article argues that improv stage dynamics translate into practical leadership skills for cross-cultural teams, especially in Tokyo&#x27;s multinational workspace. It emphasizes listening, adaptability, and a &#x27;yes, and&#x27; mindset to bridge communication gaps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出即兴表演的互动模式能落地为跨文化团队的领导技巧，尤其在东京的多元工作环境中更具价值。强调倾听、快速适应以及跟进式的“先接受再扩展”思维以缩小沟通鸿沟。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.tokyodev.com/tokyodev-production-r2/variants/q2e92zkkbfd6jnhr0lzcdgwh05ff/956d624b8ede2501210d1c5f4f0e478cb7c422176ad791a8bd107293008e7d14' data-full='https://assets.tokyodev.com/tokyodev-production-r2/variants/q2e92zkkbfd6jnhr0lzcdgwh05ff/956d624b8ede2501210d1c5f4f0e478cb7c422176ad791a8bd107293008e7d14' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49323459' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49323459' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(392) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ivan-gavran.github.io/0-social-processes-paper' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Case Against Formal Verification, 50 Years Later</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对形式化验证的质疑：50年后</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 13:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49323459' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49323459' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49323459'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fifty years on, the case argues formal verification has not become a practical safety net for general software. Proof-based approaches remain costly, brittle, and limited in scope, while robust software engineering hinges on testing, observability, and social processes. The piece calls for rethinking verification as a complement to engineering rather than a universal solution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在过去的半个世纪里，形式化验证并未成为普遍适用的软件安全网。证明方法成本高、易碎且适用范围有限，而健壮的软件工程更依赖测试、可观测性和工程文化。文章提倡把形式化验证作为对工程实践的补充，而非万能力解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ivan-gavran.github.io/assets/images/forall.png' data-full='https://ivan-gavran.github.io/assets/images/forall.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49286030' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49286030' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(393) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Time to Move On: Querying Without Nulls and Bags</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>告别空值与集合袋语义的查询时代</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 06:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49286030' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49286030' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49286030'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A scholarly note argues moving beyond NULLs and bag semantics in querying, proposing cleaner data modeling and alternative query strategies. The discussion has implications for SQL ergonomics and modern data modeling practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇学术笔记提出在查询中摆脱 NULL 与袋语义，倡导更清晰的数据建模与替代查询策略，对 SQL 易用性和现代数据建模实践具有影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49294025' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49294025' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(394) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/policy/979852/that-is-not-acceptable-judge-orders-google-to-make-rival-app-store-installs-easier' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Not acceptable&#x27;: Judge orders Google to make rival app store installs easier</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>“不可接受”裁定：法院命令谷歌简化对手应用商店安装</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 19:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49294025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49294025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49294025'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A court ruling requires Google to ease the process for installing apps from rival app stores, pushing for interoperability and fair competition in mobile ecosystems. The decision could shift developer and device-maker strategies around app distribution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法院裁定要求谷歌简化来自对手应用商店的应用安装流程，推动移动生态的互操作性与公平竞争。此举可能改变开发者和设备厂商在应用分发方面的策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/25047560/236883_Epic_Vs_Google_D_CVirginia.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/25047560/236883_Epic_Vs_Google_D_CVirginia.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49274894' data-tags='DevOps,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49274894' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(395) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.githubstatus.com/incidents/76t89hbfb09h' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wednesday, August 12: GitHub, Incident with Pull Requests and Issues</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>8月12日（星期三）：GitHub PR 与 Issues 事故</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 09:22 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-title'>(403) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://marc-b-reynolds.github.io/math/2026/08/10/IntDivByFP.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Moving integer division to floating-point is trivial</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将整数除法移到浮点运算其实很简单</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 10, 2026 / 22:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49253831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49253831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49253831'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Comparing integer division with floating-point reveals that, in many algorithms, a simple FP-based approach reduces edge-case handling and may improve readability, albeit with precision tradeoffs to consider.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将整数除法改为浮点除法在不少算法中可简化边界处理和代码可读性，尽管需权衡精度与性能的取舍。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(405) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/JustVugg/lumabri' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Lumabri – Run Moe Models on a P2P Swarm with Colibri</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 17:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49293523' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49293523' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lumabri runs moe-models (small anime-style models) across a P2P swarm using Colibri, enabling distributed inference without centralized servers. This approach improves privacy and resilience while showcasing a path to decentralized ML tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lumabri 通过 Colibri 在点对点群体中分发运行 moe 模型，实现去中心化推断。该方案提升隐私性与鲁棒性，同时展示了实现去中心化机器学习工具的可能路径。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RSI Simulator</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 10:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49261192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49261192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49261192'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RSI Simulator: a tool to simulate RSI-based scenarios for analysis.</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 21:30 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49267818'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dropbox presents cash generation and recurring revenue that can appeal to private equity buyers looking to optimize operations or monetize via capital structure changes. The piece weighs why Dropbox could be attractive to PE firms and what that would mean for its strategic trajectory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dropbox 被认为是私募股权收购的有吸引力对象，凭借稳定现金流和订阅收入，潜在通过运营优化或资本结构调整来改善价值。文章评估 PE 机构的利弊及其对 Dropbox 战略与估值的潜在影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49298088' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49298088' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(408) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hashagent.pages.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HashAgent – Share an AI agent as a URL, runs locally via WebGPU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HashAgent：以 URL 分享 AI 代理，本地通过 WebGPU 运行</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 05:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49298088' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49298088' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49298088'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HashAgent lets you publish an AI agent as a shareable URL that runs entirely in your browser, using WebGPU for acceleration and keeping data local. It reduces central hosting risks, boosts privacy, and enables quick sharing of interactive AI agents without server-side inference.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HashAgent 允许将一个 AI 代理以可分享的链接发布，在本地浏览器中运行并依靠 WebGPU 加速，数据不离开本机。此方式降低了对服务器端推断的依赖，提升隐私保护，同时方便快速分享可交互的 AI 代理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hashagent.pages.dev/og-card-v2.png?v=20260809' data-full='https://hashagent.pages.dev/og-card-v2.png?v=20260809' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国石油储备降至极低水平，储油洞穴可能受损</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 05:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49319566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49319566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49319566'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The U.S. Strategic Petroleum Reserve is so depleted that its storage caverns risk structural damage, potentially affecting energy security and emergency stockpile readiness. The situation underscores vulnerabilities in long-term energy resilience and price dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国战略石油储备降至极低水平，储油洞穴可能因为结构应力而受损，影响应急储备与能源安全。凸显长期能源韧性的脆弱性及市场影响因素。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>面向合同级别的 LLM 生成 GPU 内核验证器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 14, 2026 / 09:57 PT</span></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49301417'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A contract-grade verifier for LLM-generated GPU kernels demonstrates formal methods to certify correctness of AI-generated code, raising confidence in deployment. It highlights safety in ML-assisted code generation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>面向 LLM 生成的 GPU 内核的合同级别验证器展示了用于证明正确性的形式方法，提升对 AI 生成代码在实际部署中的信心和安全性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49314884' data-tags='Health,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49314884' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(417) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sugartrack-beta.vercel.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SugarTrack – an offline Android logbook for blood sugar (no account, no cloud)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SugarTrack：一款离线的 Android 血糖日志本（无需账户、无需云端）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 15, 2026 / 15:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49314884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49314884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49314884'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SugarTrack is an offline Android logbook for blood sugar that stores data locally with no account or cloud required. It emphasizes privacy and portability, with features for logging, trends, and export.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SugarTrack 是一款离线 Android 血糖日志应用，不需要账号或云端存储，强调隐私与数据可携带性，并支持日志记录、趋势查看与导出。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(418) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medicalxpress.com/news/2026-07-brain-rem-eeg.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Brain turns listening inward during REM sleep, EEG recordings suggest</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>REM 睡眠时大脑向内聆听，脑电图显示</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 15, 2026 / 06:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49310248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49310248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49310248'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>REM sleep involves the brain turning inward to process internal sounds, hinting at offline replay and memory consolidation. These findings deepen our understanding of sleep-dependent learning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>REM 睡眠中大脑向内处理内部声音，暗示离线回放与记忆巩固的作用。这些发现加深了对睡眠驱动学习的理解。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份庞大的动物照片档案显示已有约18,000种物种，且数量仍在增加。该档案由 Joel Sartore 多年坚持建立，是生物多样性研究与保护故事的宝贵资料，生动呈现地球生命的规模。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New neutrino observations through Earth offer fresh constraints on mantle composition and convection patterns, refining geophysics models. The work shows how particle physics can illuminate inaccessible parts of our planet.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过地球深部穿透的中微子观测，为地幔的成分与对流模式提供新线索，完善地球物理模型。此类粒子物理方法让人们在无法直接取样的深部地球也能获取信息。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(438) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/person-hides-prompt-injection-in-legal-filing-telling-ai-to-side-with-them/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Person Hides Prompt Injection in Legal Filing Telling AI to Side with Them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在法律文书中隐藏提示注入：让 AI 站在他们这边</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 13, 2026 / 12:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49290521' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49290521' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49290521'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A case of prompt injection embedded in a legal filing to influence AI output, illustrating new attack vectors in AI-assisted lawyering and the need for input sanitization, provenance, and auditing. It underscores why organizations should treat prompts as data and apply strict controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一例在法律文书中嵌入提示注入以左右 AI 输出的事件。此事暴露了 AI 辅助法律工作流程的新攻击向量，需要对输入进行净化、溯源和审计。进一步强调应将提示视为数据并实施严格的控制。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49231586' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49231586' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(439) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/2026/08/06/a-partial-digestion-of-the-hrt-counterexample/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A partial digestion of the HRT counterexample</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对 HRT 反例的部分解析</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 07:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49231586' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49231586' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49231586'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Terry Tao offers partial digestion of the HRT counterexample, clarifying which components hold and which fail, and providing intuition for related problems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特雷·陶（Tao）对 HRT 反例给出部分解析，阐明哪些要素成立，哪些要素不成立，并提供相关问题的直觉性理解。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49177765' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(442) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02478' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Solving the Shortest Vector Problem in $2^{0.6039n}$ Time via Mid-Point Hessian</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过中点 Hessian 的方法在 2^{0.6039n} 时间内解决最短向量问题</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 04, 2026 / 19:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49177765' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49177765' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49177765'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new approach solves the Shortest Vector Problem in roughly 2^{0.6039n} time using a mid-point Hessian technique, advancing lattice-based cryptanalysis and potentially tightening security margins for lattice-based cryptosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出一种新方法，利用中点 Hessian 技术以约 2^{0.6039n} 的时间解决最短向量问题，推动格基密码分析并可能收紧基于格的加密系统的安全裕度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(448) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.asml.com/en/company/stories/2023/how-immersion-lithography-saved-moores-law' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Immersion lithography saved Moore&#x27;s Law (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>浸没式光刻在2023年拯救了摩尔定律</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 07, 2026 / 05:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49209433'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Immersion lithography helped sustain transistor scaling in 2023 by enabling finer features while EUV rollout matured slowly. The piece underscores how continued improvement in existing lithography methods kept progress alive.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>浸没式光刻在 2023 年帮助维持晶体管微缩的步伐，使更小的特征尺寸成为可能，尽管 EUV 产线推进尚需时间。文章强调现有光刻技术的持续改进对维持制程进展的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 12, 2026 / 17:01 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: Ballet automates workflow creation by generating integrations against any API, lowering the barrier to building data pipelines and app integrations. It translates API specs into executable workflows, enabling faster integration development.</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 00:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49254376' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49254376' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49254376'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2001 paper explores the math and physics behind rainbows and glories, applying wave optics and scattering theory to explain angular patterns and color separation. It’s a classic cross-section of mathematical physics and atmospheric optics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2001 年的论文结合波动光学与散射理论解释彩虹与辉光的形成机制，属于数学物理在大气光学中的经典案例。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(457) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1083985/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Debugging Information for Inlined Functions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>内联函数的调试信息</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 09, 2026 / 06:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49231315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49231315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49231315'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article explains how debugging information for inlined functions is represented, and what information compilers emit to make sense of inlining. It covers techniques to preserve useful stack traces and variable scopes despite inlining, improving post-mortem debugging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨了内联函数在调试信息中的表示方式，以及编译器如何输出调试信息以便在优化后仍能追踪源代码。介绍了在内联导致栈帧和变量作用域变化时，调试工具应如何保持有用的调试信息。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(460) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/08/14/nvidia-discloses-21-billion-stake-in-spacex-at-end-of-second-quarter.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia discloses $21B stake in SpaceX at end of second quarter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英伟达披露在 SpaceX 的210亿美元持股</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 16, 2026 / 11:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49322519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49322519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49322519'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA reveals a $21 billion stake in SpaceX at end of Q2, signaling strategic alignment between AI hardware players and the broader space/rocket ecosystem. The disclosure hints at potential collaboration on AI-enabled launches, data processing, and cloud-like capabilities around SpaceX’s operations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英伟达在第二季度末披露持有 SpaceX 210 亿美元的股份，显示出 AI 硬件厂商与太空/航天生态之间的潜在协同。此披露可能预示双方在 AI 加持的发射、数据处理及云计算能力等方面的潜在合作。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Rightmove 之前的 Cosmorama：伦敦的房产信息创新</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 11, 2026 / 08:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49260268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49260268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49260268'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cosmorama was an early London property display system that foreshadowed modern real estate portals, visually presenting listings to the public. The piece situates this forgotten innovation within the lineage of property data and search experiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cosmorama 是伦敦早期的房产信息展示系统，可视化呈现房源信息，成为现代房地产门户的前身。文章把这项被遗忘的创新置于房产数据、城市迁移与搜索体验的发展脉络中。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archie G. Norcross&#x27; Maine Forest Fire Maps (1918–22) reveal early 20th-century fire geography and mapping methods. The collection offers historical data for ecology and risk planning, illustrating how communities tracked wildfires before modern GIS.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Archie G. Norcross 的缅因州森林火灾地图（1918–1922）展示了早期火灾地理与制图方法。该收藏为历史生态和风险规划提供数据，体现了在现代 GIS 出现前人们如何记录与理解野火。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Road to Nowhere in the Great Smoky Mountains has a deep history tied to park politics, infrastructure ambitions, and scenic access. The piece explains why the road was built, why it remains contentious, and how it shaped the region.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大烟山国家公园的‘通往无处的路’有着深厚的历史根源，涉及公园政治、基础设施野心与游客通达。文章解读这条路为何建成、为何引发争议，以及它如何影响了区域发展。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>实时追踪系统监控通过霍尔木兹海峡的船舶动向，提供近实时的密度、航线与关口动态。此类海事数据可视化增强物流洞察与地缘政治风险评估。</p>
